1→"""State filtering, ignore rules, and finding pattern matching."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import fnmatch
     6→import re
     7→
     8→__all__ = [
     9→    "finding_in_scan_scope",
    10→    "open_scope_breakdown",
    11→    "path_scoped_findings",
    12→    "is_ignored",
    13→    "matched_ignore_pattern",
    14→    "remove_ignored_findings",
    15→    "add_ignore",
    16→    "make_finding",
    17→]
    18→
    19→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    20→    Finding,
    21→    StateModel,
    22→    ensure_state_defaults,
    23→    utc_now,
    24→    validate_state_invariants,
    25→)
    26→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel
    27→
    28→
    29→def path_scoped_findings(
    30→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    31→    scan_path: str | None,
    32→) -> dict[str, Finding]:
    33→    """Filter findings to those within the given scan path."""
    34→    return {
    35→        finding_id: finding
    36→        for finding_id, finding in findings.items()
    37→        if finding_in_scan_scope(str(finding.get("file", "")), scan_path)
    38→    }
    39→
    40→
    41→def finding_in_scan_scope(file_path: str, scan_path: str | None) -> bool:
    42→    """Return True when a file path belongs to the active scan scope."""
    43→    if not scan_path or scan_path == ".":
    44→        return True
    45→    prefix = scan_path.rstrip("/") + "/"
    46→    return (
    47→        file_path.startswith(prefix)
    48→        or file_path == scan_path
    49→        or file_path == "."
    50→    )
    51→
    52→
    53→def open_scope_breakdown(
    54→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    55→    scan_path: str | None,
    56→    *,
    57→    detector: str | None = None,
    58→) -> dict[str, int]:
    59→    """Return open-finding counts split by in-scope vs out-of-scope carryover."""
    60→    in_scope = 0
    61→    out_of_scope = 0
    62→
    63→    for finding in findings.values():
    64→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    65→            continue
    66→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    67→            continue
    68→        if detector is not None and finding.get("detector") != detector:
    69→            continue
    70→        file_path = str(finding.get("file", ""))
    71→        if finding_in_scan_scope(file_path, scan_path):
    72→            in_scope += 1
    73→        else:
    74→            out_of_scope += 1
    75→
    76→    return {
    77→        "in_scope": in_scope,
    78→        "out_of_scope": out_of_scope,
    79→        "global": in_scope + out_of_scope,
    80→    }
    81→
    82→
    83→def is_ignored(finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]) -> bool:
    84→    """Check if a finding matches any ignore pattern (glob, ID prefix, or file path)."""
    85→    return matched_ignore_pattern(finding_id, file, ignore_patterns) is not None
    86→
    87→
    88→def matched_ignore_pattern(
    89→    finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]
    90→) -> str | None:
    91→    """Return the ignore pattern that matched, if any."""
    92→    for pattern in ignore_patterns:
    93→        if "*" in pattern:
    94→            target = finding_id if "::" in pattern else file
    95→            if fnmatch.fnmatch(target, pattern):
    96→                return pattern
    97→            continue
    98→
    99→        if "::" in pattern:
   100→            if finding_id.startswith(pattern):
   101→                return pattern
   102→            continue
   103→
   104→        if file == pattern or file == rel(pattern):
   105→            return pattern
   106→
   107→    return None
   108→
   109→
   110→def remove_ignored_findings(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   111→    """Suppress findings matching an ignore pattern. Returns count affected."""
   112→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   113→    matched_ids = [
   114→        finding_id
   115→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
   116→        if is_ignored(finding_id, finding["file"], [pattern])
   117→    ]
   118→    now = utc_now()
   119→    for finding_id in matched_ids:
   120→        finding = state["findings"][finding_id]
   121→        finding["suppressed"] = True
   122→        finding["suppressed_at"] = now
   123→        finding["suppression_pattern"] = pattern
   124→    # Deferred import to avoid circular dependency with engine._state.scoring
   125→    from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
   126→
   127→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   128→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   129→    return len(matched_ids)
   130→
   131→
   132→def add_ignore(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   133→    """Add an ignore pattern and remove existing matching findings."""
   134→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   135→    config = state.setdefault("config", {})
   136→    ignores = config.setdefault("ignore", [])
   137→    if pattern not in ignores:
   138→        ignores.append(pattern)
   139→    return remove_ignored_findings(state, pattern)
   140→
   141→
   142→def make_finding(
   143→    detector: str,
   144→    file: str,
   145→    name: str,
   146→    *,
   147→    tier: int,
   148→    confidence: str,
   149→    summary: str,
   150→    detail: dict | None = None,
   151→) -> Finding:
   152→    """Create a normalized finding dict with a stable ID."""
   153→    rfile = rel(file)
   154→    finding_id = f"{detector}::{rfile}::{name}" if name else f"{detector}::{rfile}"
   155→    now = utc_now()
   156→    return {
   157→        "id": finding_id,
   158→        "detector": detector,
   159→        "file": rfile,
   160→        "tier": tier,
   161→        "confidence": confidence,
   162→        "summary": summary,
   163→        "detail": detail or {},
   164→        "status": "open",
   165→        "note": None,
   166→        "first_seen": now,
   167→        "last_seen": now,
   168→        "resolved_at": None,
   169→        "reopen_count": 0,
   170→    }
   171→
   172→
   173→_HEX8_RE = re.compile(r'^[0-9a-f]{8}$')
   174→
   175→
   176→def _matches_pattern(finding_id: str, finding: dict[str, str], pattern: str) -> bool:
   177→    """Check if a finding matches by ID, glob, prefix, detector, suffix, or path."""
   178→    if finding_id == pattern:
   179→        return True
   180→    if "*" in pattern and fnmatch.fnmatch(finding_id, pattern):
   181→        return True
   182→    if "::" in pattern and finding_id.startswith(pattern):
   183→        return True
   184→    if _HEX8_RE.match(pattern) and finding_id.endswith("::" + pattern):
   185→        return True
   186→    return (
   187→        finding.get("detector") == pattern
   188→        or finding["file"] == pattern
   189→        or finding["file"].startswith(pattern.rstrip("/") + "/")
   190→    )
   191→
```

1→"""Pattern → finding-ID resolution for plan commands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel
     6→from desloppify.state import StateModel, match_findings
     7→
     8→
     9→def resolve_ids_from_patterns(
    10→    state: StateModel,
    11→    patterns: list[str],
    12→    *,
    13→    plan: PlanModel | None = None,
    14→    status_filter: str = "open",
    15→) -> list[str]:
    16→    """Resolve one or more patterns to a deduplicated list of finding IDs.
    17→
    18→    When *plan* is provided, literal IDs that exist only in the plan
    19→    (e.g. ``subjective::*`` synthetic items) are included even if they
    20→    have no corresponding entry in ``state["findings"]``.
    21→    """
    22→    seen: set[str] = set()
    23→    result: list[str] = []
    24→
    25→    # Collect all plan IDs for literal-match fallback
    26→    plan_ids: set[str] = set()
    27→    if plan is not None:
    28→        plan_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    29→        plan_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    30→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    31→            plan_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    32→
    33→    for pattern in patterns:
    34→        matches = match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter)
    35→        if matches:
    36→            for finding in matches:
    37→                fid = finding["id"]
    38→                if fid not in seen:
    39→                    seen.add(fid)
    40→                    result.append(fid)
    41→        elif pattern in plan_ids and pattern not in seen:
    42→            # Literal plan ID (e.g. subjective::foo) not in state findings
    43→            seen.add(pattern)
    44→            result.append(pattern)
    45→    return result
    46→
    47→
    48→__all__ = ["resolve_ids_from_patterns"]
    49→
```

1→"""Compact queue table renderer for ``plan queue``."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.guardrails import print_synthesis_guardrail_info
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    10→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize, print_table
    11→from desloppify.engine.plan import compute_new_finding_ids, load_plan
    12→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    13→
    14→
    15→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    16→    if len(text) <= width:
    17→        return text
    18→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"
    19→
    20→
    21→def _cluster_tier_label(item: dict) -> str:
    22→    """Compute a tier label from cluster members, e.g. 'T2' or 'T1-T3'."""
    23→    members = item.get("members", [])
    24→    if not members:
    25→        return ""
    26→    tiers = {int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) for m in members}
    27→    lo, hi = min(tiers), max(tiers)
    28→    if lo == hi:
    29→        return f"T{lo}"
    30→    return f"T{lo}-T{hi}"
    31→
    32→
    33→def _resolve_plan_context(plan: dict, cluster_filter: str | None) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None]:
    34→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    35→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("overrides") or plan.get("clusters"):
    36→        plan_data = plan
    37→
    38→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
    39→    if plan_data and not cluster_filter:
    40→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    41→        if active_cluster:
    42→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
    43→    return plan_data, effective_cluster
    44→
    45→
    46→def _print_queue_header(
    47→    *,
    48→    items: list[dict],
    49→    tier_counts: dict,
    50→    include_skipped: bool,
    51→    plan: dict,
    52→    plan_data: dict | None,
    53→    new_count: int = 0,
    54→) -> None:
    55→    total = len(items)
    56→    skipped_count = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("plan_skipped"))
    57→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    58→    tc_parts = " ".join(f"T{t}:{tier_counts.get(t, 0)}" for t in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    59→    finding_total = sum(tier_counts.get(t, 0) for t in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    60→    new_suffix = f"  ({new_count} new this scan)" if new_count > 0 else ""
    61→    if finding_total != non_skipped:
    62→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {non_skipped} entries  ({finding_total} findings: {tc_parts}){new_suffix}", "bold"))
    63→    else:
    64→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {non_skipped} items  ({tc_parts}){new_suffix}", "bold"))
    65→
    66→    focus = plan.get("active_cluster") if plan_data else None
    67→    if focus:
    68→        print(colorize(f"  Focus: {focus}", "cyan"))
    69→
    70→    if include_skipped or skipped_count != 0:
    71→        return
    72→    plan_skipped_count = len(plan.get("skipped", {})) if plan_data else 0
    73→    if not plan_skipped_count:
    74→        return
    75→    print(
    76→        colorize(
    77→            f"  ({plan_skipped_count} skipped item{'s' if plan_skipped_count != 1 else ''}"
    78→            " hidden — use --include-skipped)",
    79→            "dim",
    80→        )
    81→    )
    82→
    83→
    84→def _queue_display_items(items: list[dict], *, top: int) -> list[dict]:
    85→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
    86→        return items[:top]
    87→    return items
    88→
    89→
    90→def _build_rows(display_items: list[dict], new_ids: set[str] | None = None) -> list[list[str]]:
    91→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
    92→    _new = new_ids or set()
    93→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
    94→        pos = str(idx)
    95→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
    96→
    97→        if kind == "cluster":
    98→            tier_str = _cluster_tier_label(item)
    99→            member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
   100→            detector = item.get("detector", "cluster")
   101→            new_in_cluster = sum(
   102→                1 for m in item.get("members", [])
   103→                if m.get("id") in _new
   104→            )
   105→            new_tag = f" (+{new_in_cluster} new)" if new_in_cluster else ""
   106→            summary = f"[{member_count} items{new_tag}] {item.get('summary', '')}"
   107→            cluster_name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
   108→        else:
   109→            tier_val = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   110→            tier_str = f"T{tier_val}"
   111→            detector = item.get("detector", "")
   112→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   113→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   114→            cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "") if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else ""
   115→
   116→        prefix = "* " if item.get("id") in _new else ""
   117→        suffix = " [skip]" if item.get("plan_skipped") else ""
   118→        summary_display = _truncate(prefix + summary, 48) + suffix
   119→        rows.append([pos, tier_str, detector, summary_display, cluster_name])
   120→    return rows
   121→
   122→
   123→def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   124→    """Render a compact table of all upcoming queue items."""
   125→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   126→    state = runtime.state
   127→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   128→        return
   129→
   130→    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
   131→    tier_filter = getattr(args, "tier", None)
   132→    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
   133→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
   134→
   135→    plan = load_plan()
   136→    print_synthesis_guardrail_info(plan=plan, state=state)
   137→    plan_data, effective_cluster = _resolve_plan_context(plan, cluster_filter)
   138→
   139→    queue = build_work_queue(
   140→        state,
   141→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   142→            tier=tier_filter,
   143→            count=None,
   144→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   145→            status="open",
   146→            include_subjective=True,
   147→            plan=plan_data,
   148→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   149→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   150→            collapse_clusters=True,
   151→        ),
   152→    )
   153→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   154→    tier_counts = queue.get("tier_counts", {})
   155→
   156→    sort_by = getattr(args, "sort", "priority")
   157→    all_new_ids: set[str] = queue.get("new_ids", set())
   158→    # Merge review-based new finding IDs (since last synthesis)
   159→    review_new_ids = compute_new_finding_ids(plan, state)
   160→    all_new_ids = all_new_ids | review_new_ids
   161→    item_ids = {it.get("id") for it in items}
   162→    new_ids = all_new_ids & item_ids
   163→
   164→    if sort_by == "recent":
   165→        items = sorted(items, key=lambda it: it.get("first_seen", ""), reverse=True)
   166→
   167→    _print_queue_header(
   168→        items=items,
   169→        tier_counts=tier_counts,
   170→        include_skipped=include_skipped,
   171→        plan=plan,
   172→        plan_data=plan_data,
   173→        new_count=len(new_ids),
   174→    )
   175→
   176→    if not items:
   177→        print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))
   178→        return
   179→
   180→    # Determine which items to show
   181→    display_items = _queue_display_items(items, top=top)
   182→    headers = ["#", "Tier", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]
   183→    rows = _build_rows(display_items, new_ids=new_ids)
   184→
   185→    print()
   186→    widths = [4, 4, 12, 50, 16]
   187→    print_table(headers, rows, widths=widths)
   188→
   189→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   190→        remaining = len(items) - top
   191→        print(colorize(
   192→            f"\n  ... and {remaining} more (use --top 0 to show all)", "dim"
   193→        ))
   194→    print()
   195→
   196→
   197→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_queue"]
   198→

1→"""State resolution operations (match + resolve)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import copy
     6→
     7→__all__ = [
     8→    "coerce_assessment_score",
     9→    "match_findings",
    10→    "resolve_findings",
    11→]
    12→
    13→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import _matches_pattern
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    16→    StateModel,
    17→    ensure_state_defaults,
    18→    utc_now,
    19→    validate_state_invariants,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
    22→
    23→
    24→def coerce_assessment_score(value: object) -> float | None:
    25→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float.
    26→
    27→    Returns ``None`` when the value cannot be interpreted as a numeric score
    28→    (e.g. bools, non-numeric strings, missing keys).
    29→    """
    30→    if is_numeric(value):
    31→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))), 1)
    32→    if isinstance(value, dict):
    33→        raw = value.get("score")
    34→        if not is_numeric(raw):
    35→            return None
    36→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(raw))), 1)
    37→    return None
    38→
    39→
    40→def _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
    41→    state: StateModel,
    42→    *,
    43→    status: str,
    44→    resolved_findings: list[dict],
    45→    now: str,
    46→) -> None:
    47→    """Mark subjective assessments as stale when review findings are resolved.
    48→
    49→    The assessment score is preserved (not zeroed) — only a fresh review import
    50→    should change dimension scores.  The stale marker tells the UI to prompt
    51→    for a re-review.
    52→    """
    53→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    54→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    55→        return
    56→
    57→    touched_dimensions: set[str] = set()
    58→    for finding in resolved_findings:
    59→        if finding.get("detector") != "review":
    60→            continue
    61→        dimension = str(finding.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")).strip()
    62→        if dimension:
    63→            touched_dimensions.add(dimension)
    64→
    65→    for dimension in sorted(touched_dimensions):
    66→        if dimension not in assessments:
    67→            continue
    68→
    69→        payload = assessments.get(dimension)
    70→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
    71→            payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    72→            payload["refresh_reason"] = f"review_finding_{status}"
    73→            payload["stale_since"] = now
    74→        else:
    75→            assessments[dimension] = {
    76→                "score": coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0,
    77→                "needs_review_refresh": True,
    78→                "refresh_reason": f"review_finding_{status}",
    79→                "stale_since": now,
    80→            }
    81→
    82→
    83→def match_findings(
    84→    state: StateModel, pattern: str, status_filter: str = "open"
    85→) -> list[dict]:
    86→    """Return findings matching *pattern* with the given status."""
    87→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
    88→    return [
    89→        finding
    90→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
    91→        if not finding.get("suppressed")
    92→        if (status_filter == "all" or finding["status"] == status_filter)
    93→        and _matches_pattern(finding_id, finding, pattern)
    94→    ]
    95→
    96→
    97→def resolve_findings(
    98→    state: StateModel,
    99→    pattern: str,
   100→    status: str,
   101→    note: str | None = None,
   102→    attestation: str | None = None,
   103→) -> list[str]:
   104→    """Set finding status for matches and return affected finding IDs."""
   105→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   106→    now = utc_now()
   107→    resolved: list[str] = []
   108→    resolved_findings: list[dict] = []
   109→    status_filter = "all" if status == "open" else "open"
   110→    for finding in match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter):
   111→        previous_status = str(finding.get("status", "open")).strip() or "open"
   112→        if status == "open" and previous_status == "open":
   113→            continue
   114→
   115→        extra_updates: dict[str, object] = {}
   116→        if status == "wontfix":
   117→            snapshot_scan_count = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   118→            extra_updates["wontfix_scan_count"] = snapshot_scan_count
   119→            extra_updates["wontfix_snapshot"] = {
   120→                "captured_at": now,
   121→                "scan_count": snapshot_scan_count,
   122→                "tier": finding.get("tier"),
   123→                "confidence": finding.get("confidence"),
   124→                "detail": copy.deepcopy(finding.get("detail", {})),
   125→            }
   126→        if status == "open":
   127→            finding["reopen_count"] = int(finding.get("reopen_count", 0) or 0) + 1
   128→            finding.pop("wontfix_scan_count", None)
   129→            finding.pop("wontfix_snapshot", None)
   130→            previous_note = finding.get("note")
   131→            next_note = note if note is not None else previous_note
   132→            extra_updates["resolved_at"] = None
   133→            extra_updates["note"] = next_note
   134→            extra_updates["resolution_attestation"] = {
   135→                "kind": "manual_reopen",
   136→                "text": attestation or note,
   137→                "attested_at": now,
   138→                "scan_verified": False,
   139→                "previous_status": previous_status,
   140→            }
   141→
   142→        updates: dict[str, object] = {
   143→            "status": status,
   144→            "note": note,
   145→            "resolved_at": now,
   146→            "suppressed": False,
   147→            "suppressed_at": None,
   148→            "suppression_pattern": None,
   149→            "resolution_attestation": {
   150→                "kind": "manual",
   151→                "text": attestation,
   152→                "attested_at": now,
   153→                "scan_verified": False,
   154→            },
   155→        }
   156→        updates.update(extra_updates)
   157→        finding.update(updates)
   158→        resolved.append(finding["id"])
   159→        resolved_findings.append(finding)
   160→
   161→    _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
   162→        state,
   163→        status=status,
   164→        resolved_findings=resolved_findings,
   165→        now=now,
   166→    )
   167→
   168→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   169→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   170→    return resolved
   171→
```

1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import hashlib
    14→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    15→
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel
    18→
    19→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
    20→SYNTHESIS_ID = "synthesis::pending"
    21→
    22→
    23→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    24→# Result dataclasses
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26→
    27→@dataclass
    28→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    29→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    30→
    31→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    32→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    33→
    34→    @property
    35→    def changes(self) -> int:
    36→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    37→
    38→
    39→@dataclass
    40→class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    41→    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""
    42→
    43→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    44→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    45→
    46→    @property
    47→    def changes(self) -> int:
    48→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    49→
    50→
    51→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    52→# ID helpers
    53→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    54→
    55→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    56→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    57→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    58→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    59→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    60→    )
    61→
    62→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    63→    if not dim_scores:
    64→        return set()
    65→
    66→    stale: set[str] = set()
    67→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    68→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    69→            continue
    70→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    71→        if dim_key:
    72→            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    73→    return stale
    74→
    75→
    76→def _current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    77→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    78→
    79→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` directly because
    80→    ``scorecard_subjective_entries`` filters out placeholder dimensions
    81→    (they are hidden from the scorecard display).
    82→    """
    83→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    84→
    85→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    86→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    87→        return set()
    88→
    89→    unscored: set[str] = set()
    90→    for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
    91→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    92→            continue
    93→        if not payload.get("placeholder"):
    94→            continue
    95→        if dim_key:
    96→            unscored.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    97→    return unscored
    98→
    99→
   100→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   101→# Promoted-aware insertion helper
   102→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   103→
   104→def _after_promoted(order: list[str], plan: PlanModel) -> int:
   105→    """Return the insertion index just after the last promoted item in *order*.
   106→
   107→    When no promoted items are present (or none are in *order*), returns 0
   108→    so callers fall back to existing front-of-queue behavior.
   109→    """
   110→    promoted = set(plan.get("promoted_ids", []))
   111→    if not promoted:
   112→        return 0
   113→    last_idx = -1
   114→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   115→        if fid in promoted:
   116→            last_idx = i
   117→    return last_idx + 1 if last_idx >= 0 else 0
   118→
   119→
   120→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   121→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   122→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   123→
   124→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   125→    plan: PlanModel,
   126→    state: StateModel,
   127→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   128→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   129→
   130→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   131→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   132→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   133→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   134→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   135→    """
   136→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   137→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   138→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   139→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   140→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   141→
   142→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   143→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   144→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   145→        fid for fid in order
   146→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   147→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   148→        and fid not in stale_ids
   149→    ]
   150→    for fid in to_remove:
   151→        order.remove(fid)
   152→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   153→
   154→    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs after any promoted items -------------
   155→    existing = set(order)
   156→    insert_at = _after_promoted(order, plan)
   157→    for uid in reversed(sorted(unscored_ids)):
   158→        if uid not in existing:
   159→            order.insert(insert_at, uid)
   160→            result.injected.append(uid)
   161→
   162→    return result
   163→
   164→
   165→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   166→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   167→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   168→
   169→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   170→    plan: PlanModel,
   171→    state: StateModel,
   172→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   173→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale subjective dimensions.
   174→
   175→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   176→       longer stale and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned by
   177→       ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   178→    2. If no objective items remain after cleanup, inject all currently-stale
   179→       dimension IDs so the plan surfaces them as actionable work.
   180→    """
   181→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   182→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   183→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   184→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   185→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   186→
   187→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   188→    # Only prune IDs that are neither stale nor unscored.
   189→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   190→        fid for fid in order
   191→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   192→        and fid not in stale_ids
   193→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   194→    ]
   195→    for fid in to_remove:
   196→        order.remove(fid)
   197→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   198→
   199→    # --- Inject: populate when no objective items remain -----------------
   200→    has_real_items = any(fid for fid in order if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX))
   201→    if not has_real_items and stale_ids:
   202→        existing = set(order)
   203→        for sid in sorted(stale_ids):
   204→            if sid not in existing:
   205→                order.append(sid)
   206→                result.injected.append(sid)
   207→
   208→    return result
   209→
   210→
   211→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   212→# Synthesis snapshot hash + sync
   213→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   214→
   215→def review_finding_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   216→    """Hash open review finding IDs to detect changes.
   217→
   218→    Returns empty string when there are no open review findings.
   219→    """
   220→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   221→    review_ids = sorted(
   222→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   223→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   224→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   225→    )
   226→    if not review_ids:
   227→        return ""
   228→    return hashlib.sha256("|".join(review_ids).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
   229→
   230→
   231→@dataclass
   232→class SynthesisSyncResult:
   233→    """What changed during a synthesis sync."""
   234→
   235→    injected: bool = False
   236→    pruned: bool = False
   237→
   238→    @property
   239→    def changes(self) -> int:
   240→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   241→
   242→
   243→def sync_synthesis_needed(
   244→    plan: PlanModel,
   245→    state: StateModel,
   246→) -> SynthesisSyncResult:
   247→    """Inject ``synthesis::pending`` at front of queue when review findings change.
   248→
   249→    Never auto-prunes — only explicit completion (``_apply_completion``) removes it.
   250→    """
   251→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   252→    result = SynthesisSyncResult()
   253→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   254→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   255→    already_present = SYNTHESIS_ID in order
   256→
   257→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   258→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   259→
   260→    # If hash changed and not already present, inject after promoted items
   261→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash and not already_present:
   262→        order.insert(_after_promoted(order, plan), SYNTHESIS_ID)
   263→        result.injected = True
   264→
   265→    # Never auto-prune: synthesis::pending is only removed by explicit completion
   266→
   267→    return result
   268→
   269→
   270→def compute_new_finding_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   271→    """Return the set of open review/concerns finding IDs added since last synthesis.
   272→
   273→    Returns an empty set when no prior synthesis has recorded ``synthesized_ids``.
   274→    """
   275→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   276→    synthesized = set(meta.get("synthesized_ids", []))
   277→    current = {
   278→        fid for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   279→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   280→    }
   281→    return current - synthesized if synthesized else set()
   282→
   283→
   284→def is_synthesis_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   285→    """Side-effect-free check: is synthesis needed?
   286→
   287→    Returns True when ``synthesis::pending`` is in the queue OR the
   288→    review-finding snapshot hash has drifted from the last completed synthesis.
   289→    """
   290→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   291→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   292→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in order:
   293→        return True
   294→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   295→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   296→    if not last_hash:
   297→        return False
   298→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   299→    return bool(current_hash and current_hash != last_hash)
   300→
   301→
   302→__all__ = [
   303→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   304→    "SYNTHESIS_ID",
   305→    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
   306→    "SynthesisSyncResult",
   307→    "UnscoredDimensionSyncResult",
   308→    "_current_unscored_ids",
   309→    "compute_new_finding_ids",
   310→    "is_synthesis_stale",
   311→    "review_finding_snapshot_hash",
   312→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
   313→    "sync_synthesis_needed",
   314→    "sync_unscored_dimensions",
   315→]
   316→

    34→def _append_fn_pair_detail(parts: list[str], detail: dict) -> None:
    35→    if not detail.get("fn_a"):
    36→        return
    37→    a, b = detail["fn_a"], detail["fn_b"]
    38→    parts.append(f"{a['name']}:{a.get('line', '')} ↔ {b['name']}:{b.get('line', '')}")
    39→
    40→
    41→def _append_pattern_evidence(parts: list[str], detail: dict) -> None:
    42→    pattern_evidence = detail.get("pattern_evidence")
    43→    if not isinstance(pattern_evidence, dict) or not pattern_evidence:
    44→        return
    45→    summary_parts = []
    46→    for pattern_name, hits in pattern_evidence.items():
    47→        if isinstance(hits, list):
    48→            summary_parts.append(f"{pattern_name}:{len(hits)} file(s)")
    49→    if summary_parts:
    50→        parts.append(f"evidence: {', '.join(summary_parts)}")
    51→
    52→
    53→def format_detail(detail: dict) -> list[str]:
    54→    """Build display parts from a finding's detail dict."""
    55→    parts: list[str] = []
    56→    for key, label, formatter in DETAIL_DISPLAY:
    57→        value = detail.get(key)
    58→        if value is None or value == 0:
    59→            if key == "importers" and value is not None:
    60→                parts.append(f"{label}: {value}")
    61→            continue
    62→        parts.append(f"{label}: {formatter(value) if formatter else value}")
    63→
    64→    _append_fn_pair_detail(parts, detail)
    65→    _append_pattern_evidence(parts, detail)
    66→
    67→    return parts
    68→
    69→
    70→def suppressed_match_estimate(pattern: str, hidden_by_detector: dict[str, int]) -> int:
    71→    """Estimate hidden-match count for a show pattern using detector-level noise totals."""
    72→    if not isinstance(pattern, str) or not isinstance(hidden_by_detector, dict):
    73→        return 0
    74→    detector = pattern.split("::", 1)[0]
    75→    return int(hidden_by_detector.get(detector, 0))
    76→
    77→
    78→__all__ = ["DETAIL_DISPLAY", "format_detail", "suppressed_match_estimate"]
    79→

    26→def write_show_output_file(output_file: str, payload: dict, surfaced_count: int) -> bool:
    27→    """Write serialized show payload to file."""
    28→    try:
    29→        safe_write_text(output_file, json.dumps(payload, indent=2) + "\n")

    34→        return False

    38→def group_matches_by_file(matches: list[dict]) -> list[tuple[str, list]]:
    39→    """Group findings by file and sort by descending count."""
    40→    by_file: dict[str, list] = defaultdict(list)
    41→    for finding in matches:

    43→    return sorted(by_file.items(), key=lambda item: -len(item[1]))

    46→def _print_single_finding(finding: dict, *, show_code: bool) -> None:
    47→    """Render a single finding to terminal."""
    48→    normalized_status = canonical_finding_status(finding.get("status"))
    49→    status_icon = {
    50→        "open": "○",

    54→        "auto_resolved": "◌",

    56→    zone = finding.get("zone", "production")

    58→    print(
    59→        f"    {status_icon} T{finding['tier']} [{finding['confidence']}] {finding['summary']}{zone_tag}"

    61→
    62→    detail_parts = format_detail(finding.get("detail", {}))

1→"""CLI entry point: parse args, load shared context, dispatch command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→import sys
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.app.cli_support.parser import create_parser as _create_parser
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.registry import get_command_handlers
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import LangResolutionError, resolve_lang
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import CommandRuntime
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    14→from desloppify.core.registry import detector_names, on_detector_registered
    15→from desloppify.core.config import load_config
    16→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import set_exclusions
    17→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    18→from desloppify.core.paths_api import get_default_path, get_project_root
    19→from desloppify.core.runtime_state import runtime_scope
    20→from desloppify.languages import available_langs
    21→from desloppify.state import load_state
    22→
    23→_DETECTOR_NAMES: list[str] | None = None
    24→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    25→# Backward-compatible test patch hook; runtime path resolution uses get_project_root().
    26→PROJECT_ROOT = get_project_root()
    27→_PROJECT_ROOT_SENTINEL = PROJECT_ROOT
    28→
    29→
    30→def _get_detector_names() -> list[str]:
    31→    """Return cached detector names, computing on first access."""
    32→    global _DETECTOR_NAMES
    33→    if _DETECTOR_NAMES is None:
    34→        _DETECTOR_NAMES = detector_names()
    35→    return _DETECTOR_NAMES
    36→
    37→
    38→def _invalidate_detector_names_cache() -> None:
    39→    """Invalidate detector-name cache when runtime registrations change."""
    40→    global _DETECTOR_NAMES
    41→    _DETECTOR_NAMES = None
    42→
    43→
    44→on_detector_registered(_invalidate_detector_names_cache)
    45→
    46→def create_parser():
    47→    """Return the top-level argparse parser."""
    48→    return _create_parser(langs=available_langs(), detector_names=_get_detector_names())
    49→
    50→
    51→def _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config: dict):
    52→    """Merge CLI --exclude with persisted config.exclude and apply globally."""
    53→    cli_exclusions = getattr(args, "exclude", None) or []
    54→    persisted = config.get("exclude", [])
    55→    combined = list(cli_exclusions) + [e for e in persisted if e not in cli_exclusions]
    56→    if not combined:
    57→        return
    58→    set_exclusions(combined)
    59→    if cli_exclusions:
    60→        print(
    61→            colorize(f"  Excluding: {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"),
    62→            file=sys.stderr,
    63→        )
    64→        return
    65→    print(
    66→        colorize(
    67→            f"  Excluding (from config): {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"
    68→        ),
    69→        file=sys.stderr,
    70→    )
    71→
    72→
    73→def _resolve_default_path(args) -> None:
    74→    """Fill args.path from detected language or default source path.
    75→
    76→    For the review command, the last scan path (stored in state) is used as the
    77→    default so that ``desloppify review --prepare`` works on the same scope as
    78→    the preceding scan even when the project files are not under ``src/``.
    79→    """
    80→    if getattr(args, "path", None) is not None:
    81→        return
    82→    runtime_root = (
    83→        PROJECT_ROOT.resolve()
    84→        if PROJECT_ROOT is not _PROJECT_ROOT_SENTINEL and isinstance(PROJECT_ROOT, Path)
    85→        else get_project_root()
    86→    )
    87→    if getattr(args, "command", None) == "review":
    88→        try:
    89→            state_file = state_path(args)
    90→            if state_file:
    91→                saved = load_state(state_file)
    92→                saved_path = saved.get("scan_path")
    93→                if saved_path:
    94→                    args.path = str((runtime_root / saved_path).resolve())
    95→                    return
    96→        except (OSError, KeyError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as exc:
    97→            logger.debug("Failed to resolve default path from saved state: %s", exc)
    98→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    99→    if lang:
   100→        args.path = str(runtime_root / lang.default_src)
   101→    else:
   102→        args.path = str(get_default_path())
   103→
   104→
   105→def _load_shared_runtime(args) -> None:
   106→    """Load config/state and attach shared objects to parsed args."""
   107→    config = load_config()
   108→
   109→    state_file = state_path(args)
   110→    state = load_state(state_file)
   111→    _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config)
   112→
   113→    args.runtime = CommandRuntime(config=config, state=state, state_path=state_file)
   114→
   115→
   116→def _resolve_handler(command: str):
   117→    return get_command_handlers()[command]
   118→
   119→
   120→def _handle_help_command(args, parser) -> None:
   121→    """Handle explicit help command when present in parser config."""
   122→    topic = list(getattr(args, "topic", []) or [])
   123→    try:
   124→        parser.parse_args([*topic, "--help"])
   125→    except SystemExit:
   126→        return
   127→
   128→
   129→def main() -> None:
   130→    # Ensure Unicode output works on Windows terminals (cp1252 etc.)
   131→    for stream in (sys.stdout, sys.stderr):
   132→        if hasattr(stream, "reconfigure"):
   133→            try:
   134→                stream.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
   135→            except (AttributeError, OSError):
   136→                logger.debug(
   137→                    "Skipping stream reconfigure for %s (not supported)",
   138→                    getattr(stream, "name", "<stream>"),
   139→                )
   140→
   141→    parser = create_parser()
   142→    args = parser.parse_args()
   143→    if args.command == "help":
   144→        _handle_help_command(args, parser)
   145→        return
   146→
   147→    try:
   148→        with runtime_scope():
   149→            _resolve_default_path(args)
   150→            _load_shared_runtime(args)
   151→
   152→            handler = _resolve_handler(args.command)
   153→            handler(args)
   154→    except LangResolutionError as exc:
   155→        print(colorize(f"  {exc.message}", "red"), file=sys.stderr)
   156→        sys.exit(1)
   157→    except KeyboardInterrupt:
   158→        print("\nInterrupted.")
   159→        sys.exit(1)
   160→
   161→
   162→if __name__ == "__main__":
   163→    main()
   164→
```

1272→def _validate_attestation(
  1273→    attestation: str,
  1274→    stage: str,
  1275→    *,
  1276→    dimensions: list[str] | None = None,
  1277→    cluster_names: list[str] | None = None,
  1278→) -> str | None:
  1279→    """Return error message if attestation doesn't reference required data, else None."""
  1280→    text = attestation.lower()
  1281→
  1282→    if stage == "observe":
  1283→        if dimensions:
  1284→            found = [d for d in dimensions if d.lower().replace("_", " ") in text or d.lower() in text]
  1285→            if not found:
  1286→                dim_list = ", ".join(dimensions[:6])
  1287→                return f"Attestation must reference at least one dimension from the summary. Mention one of: {dim_list}"
  1288→
  1289→    elif stage == "reflect":
  1290→        refs: list[str] = []
  1291→        if dimensions:
  1292→            refs.extend(d for d in dimensions if d.lower().replace("_", " ") in text or d.lower() in text)
  1293→        if cluster_names:
  1294→            refs.extend(n for n in cluster_names if n.lower() in text)
  1295→        if not refs and (dimensions or cluster_names):
  1296→            hints = (dimensions or [])[:4] + (cluster_names or [])[:4]
  1297→            return f"Attestation must reference at least one dimension or cluster. Mention one of: {', '.join(hints)}"
  1298→
  1299→    elif stage == "organize":
  1300→        if cluster_names:
  1301→            found = [n for n in cluster_names if n.lower() in text]
  1302→            if not found:
  1303→                names = ", ".join(cluster_names[:6])
  1304→                return f"Attestation must reference at least one cluster from the plan. Mention one of: {names}"
  1305→
  1306→    return None
  1307→
  1308→
  1309→def _confirm_observe(args: argparse.Namespace, plan: dict, stages: dict, attestation: str | None) -> None:
  1310→    """Show observe summary and record confirmation if attestation is valid."""
  1311→    if "observe" not in stages:
  1312→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm: observe stage not recorded.", "red"))
  1313→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan synthesize --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
  1314→        return
  1315→    if stages["observe"].get("confirmed_at"):
  1316→        print(colorize("  Observe stage already confirmed.", "green"))
  1317→        return
  1318→
  1319→    # Show summary
  1320→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
  1321→    si = collect_synthesis_input(plan, runtime.state)
  1322→    obs = stages["observe"]
  1323→
  1324→    print(colorize("  Stage: OBSERVE — Analyse findings & spot contradictions", "bold"))
  1325→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 54, "dim"))
  1326→
  1327→    # Dimension breakdown
  1328→    by_dim, dim_names = _observe_dimension_breakdown(si)
  1329→
  1330→    finding_count = obs.get("finding_count", len(si.open_findings))
  1331→    print(f"  Your analysis covered {finding_count} findings across {len(by_dim)} dimensions:")
  1332→    for dim in dim_names:
  1333→        print(f"    {dim}: {by_dim[dim]} findings")
  1334→
  1335→    cited = obs.get("cited_ids", [])
  1336→    if cited:
  1337→        print(f"  You cited {len(cited)} finding IDs in your report.")
  1338→
  1339→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
  1340→        if attestation:
  1341→            print(colorize(
  1342→                f"\n  Attestation too short ({len(attestation.strip())} chars, min {_MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN}).",
  1343→                "red",
  1344→            ))
  1345→        print(colorize("\n  If satisfied, confirm:", "dim"))
  1346→        print(colorize('    desloppify plan synthesize --confirm observe --attestation "I have thoroughly reviewed..."', "dim"))
  1347→        print(colorize("  If not, continue reviewing findings before reflecting.", "dim"))
  1348→        return
  1349→
  1350→    # Validate attestation references actual data
  1351→    validation_err = _validate_attestation(attestation.strip(), "observe", dimensions=dim_names)
  1352→    if validation_err:
  1353→        print(colorize(f"\n  {validation_err}", "red"))
  1354→        return
  1355→
  1356→    # Record confirmation
  1357→    stages["observe"]["confirmed_at"] = utc_now()
  1358→    stages["observe"]["confirmed_text"] = attestation.strip()
  1359→    save_plan(plan)
  1360→    append_log_entry(plan, "synthesis_confirm_observe", actor="user",
  1361→                     detail={"attestation": attestation.strip()})
  1362→    save_plan(plan)
  1363→    print(colorize(f'  ✓ Observe confirmed: "{attestation.strip()}"', "green"))
  1364→
  1365→
  1366→def _confirm_reflect(args: argparse.Namespace, plan: dict, stages: dict, attestation: str | None) -> None:
  1367→    """Show reflect summary and record confirmation if attestation is valid."""
  1368→    if "reflect" not in stages:
  1369→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm: reflect stage not recorded.", "red"))
  1370→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan synthesize --stage reflect --report "..."', "dim"))
  1371→        return

195→def _apply_completion(args: argparse.Namespace, plan: dict, strategy: str) -> None:
   196→    """Shared completion logic: update meta, remove synthesis::pending, save."""
   197→    organized, total, clusters = _synthesis_coverage(plan)
   198→
   199→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   200→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in order:
   201→        order.remove(SYNTHESIS_ID)
   202→
   203→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   204→    state = runtime.state
   205→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   206→
   207→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   208→    meta["finding_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   209→    open_review_ids = sorted(
   210→        fid for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   211→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   212→    )
   213→    meta["synthesized_ids"] = open_review_ids
   214→    if strategy.strip().lower() != "same":
   215→        meta["strategy_summary"] = strategy
   216→    meta["trigger"] = "manual_synthesis"
   217→    meta["last_completed_at"] = utc_now()
   218→    meta["synthesis_stages"] = {}  # clear stages on completion
   219→    meta.pop("stage_refresh_required", None)
   220→    meta.pop("stage_snapshot_hash", None)
   221→
   222→    save_plan(plan)
   223→
   224→    cluster_count = len([c for c in clusters.values() if c.get("finding_ids")])
   225→    print(colorize(f"  Synthesis complete: {organized}/{total} findings in {cluster_count} cluster(s).", "green"))
   226→    effective_strategy = strategy if strategy.strip().lower() != "same" else meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   227→    if effective_strategy:
   228→        print(colorize(f"  Strategy: {effective_strategy}", "cyan"))
   229→    print(colorize("  Run `desloppify next` to start implementation.", "green"))
   230→

236→def _cmd_stage_observe(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   237→    """Record the OBSERVE stage: agent analyses themes and root causes.
   238→
   239→    No citation gate — the point is genuine analysis, not ID-stuffing.
   240→    Just requires a 100-char report describing what the agent observed.
   241→    """
   242→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   243→
   244→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   245→    state = runtime.state
   246→    plan = load_plan()
   247→
   248→    # Auto-start: inject synthesis::pending if not present
   249→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   250→        order = plan.setdefault("queue_order", [])
   251→        order.insert(0, SYNTHESIS_ID)
   252→        meta = plan.setdefault("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   253→        meta["synthesis_stages"] = {}
   254→        save_plan(plan)
   255→        print(colorize("  Synthesis auto-started (injected synthesis::pending).", "cyan"))
   256→
   257→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   258→    stages = meta.setdefault("synthesis_stages", {})
   259→    existing_stage = stages.get("observe")
   260→
   261→    # Jump-back: reuse existing report if no --report provided
   262→    is_reuse = False
   263→    if not report and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("report"):
   264→        report = existing_stage["report"]
   265→        is_reuse = True
   266→    elif not report:
   267→        print(colorize("  --report is required for --stage observe.", "red"))
   268→        print(colorize("  Write an analysis of the findings: themes, root causes, contradictions.", "dim"))
   269→        print(colorize("  Identify findings that contradict each other (opposite recommendations).", "dim"))
   270→        print(colorize("  Do NOT just list finding IDs — describe what you actually observe.", "dim"))
   271→        return
   272→
   273→    si = collect_synthesis_input(plan, state)
   274→    finding_count = len(si.open_findings)
   275→
   276→    # Edge case: 0 findings
   277→    if finding_count == 0:
   278→        stages["observe"] = {
   279→            "stage": "observe",
   280→            "report": report,
   281→            "cited_ids": [],
   282→            "timestamp": utc_now(),
   283→            "finding_count": 0,
   284→        }
   285→        if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
   286→            stages["observe"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
   287→            stages["observe"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
   288→        cleared = _cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "observe")
   289→        if not is_reuse:
   290→            stages["observe"].pop("confirmed_at", None)
   291→            stages["observe"].pop("confirmed_text", None)
   292→        save_plan(plan)
   293→        print(colorize("  Observe stage recorded (no findings to analyse).", "green"))
   294→        if is_reuse:
   295→            print(colorize("  Observe data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   296→            if cleared:
   297→                print(colorize(f"  Cleared confirmations on: {', '.join(cleared)}", "dim"))
   298→        return
   299→
   300→    # Validation: report length (no citation counting)
   301→    min_chars = 50 if finding_count <= 3 else 100
   302→    if len(report) < min_chars:
   303→        print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum {min_chars}).", "red"))
   304→        print(colorize("  Describe themes, root causes, contradictions, and how findings relate.", "dim"))
   305→        return
   306→
   307→    # Save stage (still extract citations for analytics, but don't gate on them)
   308→    valid_ids = set(si.open_findings.keys())
   309→    cited = extract_finding_citations(report, valid_ids)
   310→
   311→    stages["observe"] = {
   312→        "stage": "observe",
   313→        "report": report,
   314→        "cited_ids": sorted(cited),
   315→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
   316→        "finding_count": finding_count,
   317→    }
   318→
   319→    # Jump-back: preserve or clear confirmation
   320→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
   321→        stages["observe"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
   322→        stages["observe"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
   323→    cleared = _cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "observe")
   324→
   325→    save_plan(plan)
   326→
   327→    append_log_entry(plan, "synthesis_observe", actor="user",
   328→                     detail={"finding_count": finding_count, "cited_ids": sorted(cited),
   329→                             "reuse": is_reuse})
   330→    save_plan(plan)
   331→
   332→    print(colorize(
   333→        f"  Observe stage recorded: {finding_count} findings analysed.",
   334→        "green",
   335→    ))
   336→    if is_reuse:
   337→        print(colorize("  Observe data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   338→        if cleared:
   339→            print(colorize(f"  Cleared confirmations on: {', '.join(cleared)}", "dim"))
   340→    else:
   341→        print(colorize("  Now confirm your analysis.", "yellow"))
   342→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan synthesize --confirm observe", "dim"))

   345→def _cmd_stage_reflect(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   346→    """Record the REFLECT stage: compare current findings against completed work.
   347→
   348→    Forces the agent to consider what was previously resolved and whether

   350→    If recurring patterns are detected, the report must mention at least one
   351→    recurring dimension name.
   352→    """
   353→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   354→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   355→

345→def _cmd_stage_reflect(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   346→    """Record the REFLECT stage: compare current findings against completed work.
   347→
   348→    Forces the agent to consider what was previously resolved and whether
   349→    similar issues are recurring. Requires a 100-char report (50 if ≤3 findings).
   350→    If recurring patterns are detected, the report must mention at least one
   351→    recurring dimension name.
   352→    """
   353→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   354→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   355→
   356→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   357→    state = runtime.state
   358→    plan = load_plan()
   359→
   360→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   361→        print(colorize("  synthesis::pending is not in the queue — nothing to reflect on.", "yellow"))
   362→        return
   363→
   364→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   365→    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
   366→
   367→    # Jump-back: reuse existing report if no --report provided
   368→    existing_stage = stages.get("reflect")
   369→    is_reuse = False
   370→    if not report and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("report"):
   371→        report = existing_stage["report"]
   372→        is_reuse = True
   373→    elif not report:
   374→        print(colorize("  --report is required for --stage reflect.", "red"))
   375→        print(colorize("  Compare current findings against completed work and form a holistic strategy:", "dim"))
   376→        print(colorize("  - What clusters were previously completed? Did fixes hold?", "dim"))
   377→        print(colorize("  - Are any dimensions recurring (resolved before, open again)?", "dim"))
   378→        print(colorize("  - What contradictions did you find? Which direction will you take?", "dim"))
   379→        print(colorize("  - Big picture: what to prioritize, what to defer, what to skip?", "dim"))
   380→        return
   381→
   382→    if "observe" not in stages:
   383→        print(colorize("  Cannot reflect: observe stage not complete.", "red"))
   384→        print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan synthesize --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
   385→        return
   386→
   387→    si = collect_synthesis_input(plan, state)
   388→
   389→    # Fold-confirm: auto-confirm observe if attestation provided
   390→    if not stages["observe"].get("confirmed_at"):
   391→        if attestation and len(attestation.strip()) >= _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   392→            _by_dim, dim_names = _observe_dimension_breakdown(si)
   393→            validation_err = _validate_attestation(attestation.strip(), "observe", dimensions=dim_names)
   394→            if validation_err:
   395→                print(colorize(f"  {validation_err}", "red"))
   396→                return
   397→            stages["observe"]["confirmed_at"] = utc_now()
   398→            stages["observe"]["confirmed_text"] = attestation.strip()
   399→            save_plan(plan)
   400→            print(colorize("  \u2713 Observe auto-confirmed via --attestation.", "green"))
   401→        else:
   402→            print(colorize("  Cannot reflect: observe stage not confirmed.", "red"))
   403→            print(colorize("  Run: desloppify plan synthesize --confirm observe", "dim"))
   404→            print(colorize("  Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm observe inline.", "dim"))
   405→            return
   406→
   407→    finding_count = len(si.open_findings)
   408→
   409→    # Validation: report length
   410→    min_chars = 50 if finding_count <= 3 else 100
   411→    if len(report) < min_chars:
   412→        print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum {min_chars}).", "red"))
   413→        print(colorize("  Describe how current findings relate to previously completed work.", "dim"))
   414→        return
   415→
   416→    # Detect recurring patterns
   417→    recurring = detect_recurring_patterns(si.open_findings, si.resolved_findings)
   418→    recurring_dims = sorted(recurring.keys())
   419→
   420→    # If recurring patterns exist, report must mention at least one dimension
   421→    if recurring_dims:
   422→        report_lower = report.lower()
   423→        mentioned = [dim for dim in recurring_dims if dim.lower() in report_lower]
   424→        if not mentioned:
   425→            print(colorize("  Recurring patterns detected but not addressed in report:", "red"))
   426→            for dim in recurring_dims:
   427→                info = recurring[dim]
   428→                print(colorize(
   429→                    f"    {dim}: {len(info['resolved'])} resolved, "
   430→                    f"{len(info['open'])} still open — potential loop",
   431→                    "yellow",
   432→                ))
   433→            print(colorize(
   434→                "  Your report must mention at least one recurring dimension name.",
   435→                "dim",
   436→            ))
   437→            return
   438→
   439→    # Save stage
   440→    stages = meta.setdefault("synthesis_stages", {})
   441→    stages["reflect"] = {
   442→        "stage": "reflect",
   443→        "report": report,
   444→        "cited_ids": [],
   445→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
   446→        "finding_count": finding_count,
   447→        "recurring_dimensions": recurring_dims,
   448→    }
   449→
   450→    # Jump-back: preserve or clear confirmation
   451→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
   452→        stages["reflect"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
   453→        stages["reflect"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
   454→    cleared = _cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "reflect")
   455→
   456→    save_plan(plan)
   457→
   458→    append_log_entry(plan, "synthesis_reflect", actor="user",
   459→                     detail={"finding_count": finding_count, "recurring_dims": recurring_dims,
   460→                             "reuse": is_reuse})
   461→    save_plan(plan)
   462→
   463→    print(colorize(
   464→        f"  Reflect stage recorded: {finding_count} findings, "
   465→        f"{len(recurring_dims)} recurring dimension(s).",
   466→        "green",
   467→    ))
   468→    if is_reuse:
   469→        print(colorize("  Reflect data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   470→        if cleared:
   471→            print(colorize(f"  Cleared confirmations on: {', '.join(cleared)}", "dim"))
   472→    else:
   473→        print(colorize("  Now confirm your strategy.", "yellow"))
   474→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan synthesize --confirm reflect", "dim"))
   475→    if recurring_dims:
   476→        for dim in recurring_dims:
   477→            info = recurring[dim]
   478→            print(colorize(
   479→                f"    {dim}: {len(info['resolved'])} resolved, {len(info['open'])} still open",
   480→                "dim",
   481→            ))
   482→
   483→    # Print the reflect report prominently — this is the agent's briefing to the user
   484→    print()
   485→    print(colorize("  \u250c\u2500 Strategic briefing (share with user before organizing) \u2500\u2510", "cyan"))
   486→    for line in report.strip().splitlines():
   487→        print(colorize(f"  \u2502 {line}", "cyan"))
   488→    print(colorize("  \u2514" + "\u2500" * 57 + "\u2518", "cyan"))

   490→    print(colorize(

   493→    ))

   495→        "  - What themes and root causes you see",

   497→    ))

   501→    ))

   504→        "yellow",
   505→    ))
   506→    print(colorize(
   507→        "  Wait for their input before creating clusters.",

   509→    ))
   510→    print()
   511→    print(colorize(

1→"""Tests for 8-char hex suffix matching in _matches_pattern."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import _matches_pattern
     6→
     7→
     8→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
     9→# Helpers
    10→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    11→
    12→_SAMPLE_ID = "review::.::holistic::dim::name::f41b3eb7"
    13→_SAMPLE_FINDING = {"detector": "review", "file": "test.py"}
    14→
    15→
    16→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    17→# Suffix match tests
    18→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    19→
    20→
    21→def test_suffix_match_8char_hex():
    22→    """8-char lowercase hex suffix matches a finding ID ending with ::suffix."""
    23→    assert _matches_pattern(_SAMPLE_ID, _SAMPLE_FINDING, "f41b3eb7") is True
    24→
    25→
    26→def test_suffix_no_match_wrong_hex():
    27→    """Different 8-char hex does not match."""
    28→    assert _matches_pattern(_SAMPLE_ID, _SAMPLE_FINDING, "00000000") is False
    29→
    30→
    31→def test_suffix_no_match_too_short():
    32→    """7-char pattern is not treated as a suffix match."""
    33→    assert _matches_pattern(_SAMPLE_ID, _SAMPLE_FINDING, "f41b3eb") is False
    34→
    35→
    36→def test_suffix_no_match_non_hex():
    37→    """Pattern with non-hex chars is not treated as a suffix match."""
    38→    assert _matches_pattern(_SAMPLE_ID, _SAMPLE_FINDING, "f41b3exz") is False
    39→
    40→
    41→def test_suffix_no_match_uppercase():
    42→    """Uppercase hex is not treated as suffix match (finding IDs use lowercase)."""
    43→    assert _matches_pattern(_SAMPLE_ID, _SAMPLE_FINDING, "F41B3EB7") is False
    44→
    45→
    46→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    47→# End-to-end through match_findings
    48→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    49→
    50→
    51→def test_match_findings_via_suffix():
    52→    """Suffix matching works through the match_findings() public API."""
    53→    from desloppify.engine._state.resolution import match_findings
    54→
    55→    state = {
    56→        "findings": {
    57→            _SAMPLE_ID: {
    58→                "id": _SAMPLE_ID,
    59→                "status": "open",
    60→                "detector": "review",
    61→                "file": "test.py",
    62→                "summary": "test",
    63→                "confidence": "medium",
    64→                "tier": 2,
    65→                "detail": {},
    66→            },
    67→        },
    68→        "config": {},
    69→    }
    70→    results = match_findings(state, "f41b3eb7")
    71→    assert len(results) == 1
    72→    assert results[0]["id"] == _SAMPLE_ID
    73→
    74→
    75→def test_match_findings_suffix_no_match():
    76→    """Suffix that doesn't match any finding returns empty."""
    77→    from desloppify.engine._state.resolution import match_findings
    78→
    79→    state = {
    80→        "findings": {
    81→            _SAMPLE_ID: {
    82→                "id": _SAMPLE_ID,
    83→                "status": "open",
    84→                "detector": "review",
    85→                "file": "test.py",
    86→                "summary": "test",
    87→                "confidence": "medium",
    88→                "tier": 2,
    89→                "detail": {},
    90→            },
    91→        },
    92→        "config": {},
    93→    }
    94→    results = match_findings(state, "deadbeef")
    95→    assert len(results) == 0
    96→

533→def _cmd_stage_organize(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   534→    """Record the ORGANIZE stage: validates cluster enrichment.
   535→
   536→    Instead of gating on a text report, validates that the plan data

   538→    action_steps. This forces the agent to actually think about each
   539→    cluster's execution plan.

   542→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)

   564→        else:

   566→            print(colorize('  Run: desloppify plan synthesize --stage reflect --report "..."', "dim"))

   572→            runtime = command_runtime(args)
   573→            si = collect_synthesis_input(plan, runtime.state)
   574→            recurring = detect_recurring_patterns(si.open_findings, si.resolved_findings)
   575→            reflect_dims = list(recurring.keys()) if recurring else []
   576→            reflect_clusters = [n for n in plan.get("clusters", {}) if not plan["clusters"][n].get("auto")]
   577→            validation_err = _validate_attestation(

   592→            return
   593→
   594→    # Validate: at least 1 manual cluster with findings
   595→    manual_clusters = _manual_clusters_with_findings(plan)
   596→    if not manual_clusters:

   598→        any_clusters = [

   652→        stages["organize"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
   653→    cleared = _cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "organize")
   654→
   655→    save_plan(plan)
   656→
   657→    append_log_entry(plan, "synthesis_organize", actor="user",
   658→                     detail={"cluster_count": len(manual_clusters), "reuse": is_reuse})
   659→    save_plan(plan)
   660→
   661→    print(colorize(
   662→        f"  Organize stage recorded: {len(manual_clusters)} enriched cluster(s).",
   663→        "green",
   664→    ))
   665→    if is_reuse:
   666→        print(colorize("  Organize data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   667→        if cleared:
   668→            print(colorize(f"  Cleared confirmations on: {', '.join(cleared)}", "dim"))
   669→    else:
   670→        print(colorize("  Now confirm the plan.", "yellow"))
   671→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan synthesize --confirm organize", "dim"))
   672→    # Show cluster summary
   673→    for name in manual_clusters:
   674→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(name, {})
   675→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
   676→        desc = cluster.get("description", "")
   677→        desc_str = f" \u2014 {desc}" if desc else ""
   678→        print(colorize(f"    {name}: {len(cluster.get('finding_ids', []))} findings, {len(steps)} steps{desc_str}", "dim"))
   679→
   680→    # Print the organize report prominently — this is the debrief
   681→    print()
   682→    print(colorize("  \u250c\u2500 Prioritized organization (share with user) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2510", "cyan"))

50→def _cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages: dict, from_stage: str) -> list[str]:
    51→    """Clear confirmed_at/confirmed_text on stages AFTER *from_stage*. Returns cleared names."""
    52→    try:
    53→        idx = _STAGE_ORDER.index(from_stage)
    54→    except ValueError:
    55→        return []
    56→    cleared: list[str] = []
    57→    for later in _STAGE_ORDER[idx + 1:]:
    58→        if later in stages and stages[later].get("confirmed_at"):
    59→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_at", None)
    60→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_text", None)
    61→            cleared.append(later)
    62→    return cleared
    63→
    64→
    65→def _observe_dimension_breakdown(si) -> tuple[dict[str, int], list[str]]:
    66→    """Count findings per dimension from a SynthesisInput. Returns (by_dim, sorted_dim_names)."""
    67→    by_dim: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
    68→    for _fid, f in si.open_findings.items():
    69→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
    70→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "unknown")
    71→        by_dim[dim] += 1
    72→    dim_names = sorted(by_dim, key=lambda d: (-by_dim[d], d))
    73→    return dict(by_dim), dim_names
    74→
    75→
    76→def _synthesis_coverage(plan: dict) -> tuple[int, int, dict]:
    77→    """Return (organized, total, clusters) for synthesis progress."""
    78→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    79→    all_cluster_ids: set[str] = set()
    80→    for c in clusters.values():
    81→        all_cluster_ids.update(c.get("finding_ids", []))
    82→    queue_ids = [fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", []) if fid != SYNTHESIS_ID]
    83→    organized = sum(1 for fid in queue_ids if fid in all_cluster_ids)
    84→    return organized, len(queue_ids), clusters

    87→def _unenriched_clusters(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, list[str]]]:

    93→    gaps: list[tuple[str, list[str]]] = []
    94→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    95→        if not cluster.get("finding_ids"):
    96→            continue
    97→        if cluster.get("auto"):
    98→            continue
    99→        missing: list[str] = []
   100→        if not cluster.get("description"):
   101→            missing.append("description")
   102→        if not cluster.get("action_steps"):
   103→            missing.append("action_steps")
   104→        if missing:

   106→    return gaps

   118→    """Print the 4-stage progress indicator."""
   119→    print(colorize("  Stages:", "dim"))
   120→    for stage_name, label in SYNTHESIS_STAGE_LABELS:
   121→        if stage_name in stages:
