```
    45→
    46→
    47→_STAGE_ORDER = ["observe", "reflect", "organize"]
    48→
    49→
    50→def _cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages: dict, from_stage: str) -> list[str]:
    51→    """Clear confirmed_at/confirmed_text on stages AFTER *from_stage*. Returns cleared names."""
    52→    try:
    53→        idx = _STAGE_ORDER.index(from_stage)
    54→    except ValueError:
    55→        return []
    56→    cleared: list[str] = []
    57→    for later in _STAGE_ORDER[idx + 1:]:
    58→        if later in stages and stages[later].get("confirmed_at"):
    59→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_at", None)
    60→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_text", None)
    61→            cleared.append(later)
    62→    return cleared
    63→
    64→
    65→def _observe_dimension_breakdown(si) -> tuple[dict[str, int], list[str]]:
    66→    """Count findings per dimension from a SynthesisInput. Returns (by_dim, sorted_dim_names)."""
    67→    by_dim: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
    68→    for _fid, f in si.open_findings.items():
    69→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
    70→        dim = detail.get("dimension", "unknown")
    71→        by_dim[dim] += 1
    72→    dim_names = sorted(by_dim, key=lambda d: (-by_dim[d], d))
    73→    return dict(by_dim), dim_names
    74→
    75→
    76→def _synthesis_coverage(plan: dict) -> tuple[int, int, dict]:
    77→    """Return (organized, total, clusters) for synthesis progress."""
    78→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    79→    all_cluster_ids: set[str] = set()
    80→    for c in clusters.values():
    81→        all_cluster_ids.update(c.get("finding_ids", []))
    82→    queue_ids = [fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", []) if fid != SYNTHESIS_ID]
    83→    organized = sum(1 for fid in queue_ids if fid in all_cluster_ids)
    84→    return organized, len(queue_ids), clusters
    85→
    86→
    87→def _unenriched_clusters(plan: dict) -> list[tuple[str, list[str]]]:
    88→    """Return clusters with findings that are missing required enrichment.
    89→
    90→    Returns list of (cluster_name, list_of_missing_fields).
    91→    Only checks non-auto clusters (manual clusters the agent creates).
    92→    """
    93→    gaps: list[tuple[str, list[str]]] = []
    94→    for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    95→        if not cluster.get("finding_ids"):
    96→            continue
    97→        if cluster.get("auto"):
    98→            continue
    99→        missing: list[str] = []
   100→        if not cluster.get("description"):
   101→            missing.append("description")
   102→        if not cluster.get("action_steps"):
   103→            missing.append("action_steps")
   104→        if missing:
   105→            gaps.append((name, missing))
   106→    return gaps
   107→
   108→
   109→def _manual_clusters_with_findings(plan: dict) -> list[str]:
   110→    """Return names of non-auto clusters that have findings."""
   111→    return [
   112→        name for name, c in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   113→        if c.get("finding_ids") and not c.get("auto")
   114→    ]
   115→
   116→
   117→def _print_stage_progress(stages: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> None:
   118→    """Print the 4-stage progress indicator."""
   119→    print(colorize("  Stages:", "dim"))
   120→    for stage_name, label in SYNTHESIS_STAGE_LABELS:
   121→        if stage_name in stages:
   122→            print(colorize(f"    \u2713 {label}", "green"))
   123→        elif SYNTHESIS_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES[stage_name].issubset(stages):
   124→            print(colorize(f"    \u2192 {label} (current)", "yellow"))
   125→        else:
   126→            print(colorize(f"    \u25cb {label}", "dim"))
   127→
   128→    # Show enrichment gaps when in the organize stage
   129→    if plan and "reflect" in stages and "organize" not in stages:
   130→        gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
   131→        manual = _manual_clusters_with_findings(plan)
   132→        if not manual:
   133→            print(colorize("\n    No manual clusters yet. Create clusters and enrich them.", "yellow"))
   134→        elif gaps:
   135→            print(colorize(f"\n    {len(gaps)} cluster(s) need enrichment:", "yellow"))
   136→            for name, missing in gaps:
   137→                print(colorize(f"      {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
   138→            print(colorize(
   139→                f"      Fix: {SYNTH_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH_COMPACT}",
   140→                "dim",
   141→            ))
   142→        else:
   143→            print(colorize(f"\n    All {len(manual)} manual cluster(s) enriched.", "green"))
   144→
   145→
   146→def _print_progress(plan: dict, open_findings: dict) -> None:
   147→    """Show cluster state and unclustered findings."""
   148→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   149→    # Only show clusters that actually have findings (hide empty/stale ones)
   150→    active_clusters = {
   151→        name: c for name, c in clusters.items()
   152→        if c.get("finding_ids")
   153→    }
   154→    if active_clusters:
   155→        print(colorize("\n  Current clusters:", "cyan"))
   156→        for name, cluster in active_clusters.items():
   157→            count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   158→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   159→            steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
   160→            auto = cluster.get("auto", False)
   161→            tags: list[str] = []
   162→            if auto:
   163→                tags.append("auto")
   164→            if desc:
   165→                tags.append("desc")
   166→            else:
   167→                tags.append("no desc")
   168→            if steps:
   169→                tags.append(f"{len(steps)} steps")
   170→            else:
   171→                if not auto:
   172→                    tags.append("no steps")
   173→            tag_str = f" [{', '.join(tags)}]"
   174→            desc_str = f" \u2014 {desc}" if desc else ""
   175→            print(f"    {name}: {count} items{tag_str}{desc_str}")
   176→
   177→    all_clustered: set[str] = set()
   178→    for c in clusters.values():
   179→        all_clustered.update(c.get("finding_ids", []))
   180→    unclustered = [fid for fid in open_findings if fid not in all_clustered]
   181→    if unclustered:
   182→        print(colorize(f"\n  {len(unclustered)} findings not yet in a cluster:", "yellow"))
   183→        for fid in unclustered[:10]:
   184→            f = open_findings[fid]
   185→            dim = (f.get("detail", {}) or {}).get("dimension", "") if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else ""
   186→            short = short_finding_id(fid)
   187→            print(f"    [{short}] [{dim}] {f.get('summary', '')}")
   188→        if len(unclustered) > 10:
   189→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(unclustered) - 10} more", "dim"))
   190→    elif open_findings:
   191→        organized, total, _ = _synthesis_coverage(plan)
   192→        print(colorize(f"\n  All {organized}/{total} findings are in clusters.", "green"))
   193→
   194→
   195→def _apply_completion(args: argparse.Namespace, plan: dict, strategy: str) -> None:
   196→    """Shared completion logic: update meta, remove synthesis::pending, save."""
   197→    organized, total, clusters = _synthesis_coverage(plan)
   198→
   199→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   200→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in order:
   201→        order.remove(SYNTHESIS_ID)
   202→
   203→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   204→    state = runtime.state
   205→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   206→
   207→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   208→    meta["finding_snapshot_hash"] = current_hash
   209→    open_review_ids = sorted(
   210→        fid for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   211→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   212→    )
   213→    meta["synthesized_ids"] = open_review_ids
   214→    if strategy.strip().lower() != "same":
   215→        meta["strategy_summary"] = strategy
   216→    meta["trigger"] = "manual_synthesis"
   217→    meta["last_completed_at"] = utc_now()
   218→    meta["synthesis_stages"] = {}  # clear stages on completion
   219→    meta.pop("stage_refresh_required", None)
   220→    meta.pop("stage_snapshot_hash", None)
   221→
   222→    save_plan(plan)
   223→
   224→    cluster_count = len([c for c in clusters.values() if c.get("finding_ids")])
   225→    print(colorize(f"  Synthesis complete: {organized}/{total} findings in {cluster_count} cluster(s).", "green"))
   226→    effective_strategy = strategy if strategy.strip().lower() != "same" else meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   227→    if effective_strategy:
   228→        print(colorize(f"  Strategy: {effective_strategy}", "cyan"))
   229→    print(colorize("  Run `desloppify next` to start implementation.", "green"))
   230→
   231→
   232→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   233→# Stage handlers
   234→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   235→
   236→def _cmd_stage_observe(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   237→    """Record the OBSERVE stage: agent analyses themes and root causes.
   238→
   239→    No citation gate — the point is genuine analysis, not ID-stuffing.
   240→    Just requires a 100-char report describing what the agent observed.
   241→    """
   242→    report: str | None = getattr(args, "report", None)
   243→
   244→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   245→    state = runtime.state
   246→    plan = load_plan()
   247→
   248→    # Auto-start: inject synthesis::pending if not present
   249→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   250→        order = plan.setdefault("queue_order", [])
   251→        order.insert(0, SYNTHESIS_ID)
   252→        meta = plan.setdefault("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   253→        meta["synthesis_stages"] = {}
   254→        save_plan(plan)
   255→        print(colorize("  Synthesis auto-started (injected synthesis::pending).", "cyan"))
   256→
   257→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   258→    stages = meta.setdefault("synthesis_stages", {})
   259→    existing_stage = stages.get("observe")
   260→
   261→    # Jump-back: reuse existing report if no --report provided
   262→    is_reuse = False
   263→    if not report and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("report"):
   264→        report = existing_stage["report"]
   265→        is_reuse = True
   266→    elif not report:
   267→        print(colorize("  --report is required for --stage observe.", "red"))
   268→        print(colorize("  Write an analysis of the findings: themes, root causes, contradictions.", "dim"))
   269→        print(colorize("  Identify findings that contradict each other (opposite recommendations).", "dim"))
   270→        print(colorize("  Do NOT just list finding IDs — describe what you actually observe.", "dim"))
   271→        return
   272→
   273→    si = collect_synthesis_input(plan, state)
   274→    finding_count = len(si.open_findings)
   275→
   276→    # Edge case: 0 findings
   277→    if finding_count == 0:
   278→        stages["observe"] = {
   279→            "stage": "observe",
   280→            "report": report,
   281→            "cited_ids": [],
   282→            "timestamp": utc_now(),
   283→            "finding_count": 0,
   284→        }
   285→        if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
   286→            stages["observe"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
   287→            stages["observe"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
   288→        cleared = _cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "observe")
   289→        if not is_reuse:
   290→            stages["observe"].pop("confirmed_at", None)
   291→            stages["observe"].pop("confirmed_text", None)
   292→        save_plan(plan)
   293→        print(colorize("  Observe stage recorded (no findings to analyse).", "green"))
   294→        if is_reuse:
   295→            print(colorize("  Observe data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   296→            if cleared:
   297→                print(colorize(f"  Cleared confirmations on: {', '.join(cleared)}", "dim"))
   298→        return
   299→
   300→    # Validation: report length (no citation counting)
```

```
1→"""Epic synthesis engine — LLM-driven meta-plan for review findings.
     2→
     3→Clusters review findings by root cause, dismisses false positives,
     4→resolves contradictions, and orders by dependency. The synthesis is incremental:
     5→it updates existing synthesis-clusters rather than recreating from scratch.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→

    33→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    34→# Finding-ID citation extraction
    35→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    36→
    37→FINDING_ID_RE = re.compile(r"[a-z_]+::[a-f0-9]{8,}")
    38→
    39→
    40→def extract_finding_citations(text: str, valid_ids: set[str]) -> set[str]:
    41→    """Extract finding IDs cited in free text.
    42→
    43→    Matches full finding IDs (e.g. ``review::abcdef12``) or bare 8+ char
    44→    hex suffixes that correspond to a known finding.
    45→    """
    46→    cited: set[str] = set()
    47→    # Match full finding IDs
    48→    for match in FINDING_ID_RE.finditer(text):
    49→        candidate = match.group()
    50→        if candidate in valid_ids:
    51→            cited.add(candidate)
    52→    # Match 8+ char hex suffixes
    53→    for token in re.findall(r"[0-9a-f]{8,}", text):
    54→        for valid_id in valid_ids:
    55→            if valid_id.endswith("::" + token):
    56→                cited.add(valid_id)
    57→                break
    58→    return cited
    59→
    60→
    61→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    62→# Completed-work helpers
    63→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    64→
    65→def last_real_review_timestamp(state: dict) -> str | None:
    66→    """ISO timestamp of most recent genuine review import (not manual override/scan reset)."""
    67→    REAL_MODES = {"holistic", "per_file", "trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
    68→    audit = state.get("assessment_import_audit", [])
    69→    if isinstance(audit, list):
    70→        for entry in reversed(audit):
    71→            if isinstance(entry, dict) and entry.get("mode") in REAL_MODES:
    72→                ts = entry.get("timestamp")
    73→                if ts:
    74→                    return str(ts)
    75→    holistic = (state.get("review_cache") or {}).get("holistic")
    76→    if isinstance(holistic, dict):
    77→        return holistic.get("reviewed_at")
    78→    return None
    79→
    80→
    81→def detect_recurring_patterns(
    82→    open_findings: dict[str, dict],
    83→    resolved_findings: dict[str, dict],
    84→) -> dict[str, dict]:
    85→    """Detect dimensions with both resolved AND current open findings.
    86→
    87→    Returns ``{dimension: {"open": [ids], "resolved": [ids]}}``.
    88→    A dimension with both resolved and open findings signals a potential
    89→    loop — similar issues recur after previous fixes.
    90→    """
    91→    def _dimension(f: dict) -> str:
    92→        detail = f.get("detail", {})
    93→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
    94→            return detail.get("dimension", "")
    95→        return ""
    96→
    97→    open_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    98→    for fid, f in open_findings.items():
    99→        dim = _dimension(f)
   100→        if dim:
   101→            open_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(fid)
   102→
   103→    resolved_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   104→    for fid, f in resolved_findings.items():
   105→        dim = _dimension(f)
   106→        if dim:
   107→            resolved_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(fid)
   108→
   109→    recurring: dict[str, dict] = {}
   110→    for dim in set(open_by_dim) & set(resolved_by_dim):
   111→        recurring[dim] = {
   112→            "open": open_by_dim[dim],
   113→            "resolved": resolved_by_dim[dim],
   114→        }
   115→    return recurring

   125→
   126→    open_findings: dict[str, dict]       # id -> finding (review + concerns)

   180→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   181→# Input collection
   182→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   183→
   184→def collect_synthesis_input(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> SynthesisInput:
   185→    """Gather all data needed for the synthesis LLM prompt."""
   186→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   187→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   188→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   189→    epics = synthesis_clusters(plan)
   190→
   191→    open_review: dict[str, dict] = {}
   192→    open_mechanical: dict[str, dict] = {}
   193→    for fid, f in findings.items():
   194→        if f.get("status") != "open":
   195→            continue
   196→        if f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns"):
   197→            open_review[fid] = f
   198→        else:
   199→            open_mechanical[fid] = f
   200→
   201→    synthesized_ids = set(meta.get("synthesized_ids", []))
   202→    current_review_ids = set(open_review.keys())
   203→    new_since = current_review_ids - synthesized_ids
   204→    resolved_since = synthesized_ids - current_review_ids
   205→    previously_dismissed = list(meta.get("dismissed_ids", []))
   206→    version = int(meta.get("version", 0)) + 1
   207→
   208→    # Resolved finding objects (for REFLECT stage)
   209→    resolved_finding_objs = {
   210→        fid: findings[fid] for fid in resolved_since if fid in findings
   211→    }
   212→
   213→    # Completed clusters since last synthesis completion
   214→    last_completed = meta.get("last_completed_at", "")
   215→    all_completed: list[dict] = plan.get("completed_clusters", [])
   216→    if last_completed:
   217→        recent_completed = [
   218→            c for c in all_completed
   219→            if c.get("completed_at", "") > last_completed
   220→        ]
   221→    else:
   222→        recent_completed = list(all_completed)
   223→
   224→    return SynthesisInput(
   225→        open_findings=open_review,
   226→        mechanical_findings=open_mechanical,
   227→        existing_epics=dict(epics),
   228→        dimension_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   229→        new_since_last=new_since,
   230→        resolved_since_last=resolved_since,
   231→        previously_dismissed=previously_dismissed,
   232→        synthesis_version=version,
   233→        resolved_findings=resolved_finding_objs,
   234→        completed_clusters=recent_completed,
   235→    )
   236→
   237→
   238→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   239→# Prompt construction
   240→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   241→
   242→_SYNTHESIS_SYSTEM_PROMPT = """\
   243→You are maintaining the meta-plan for this codebase. Your goal is to produce
   244→a coherent, prioritized strategy to address ALL open review findings.
   245→
   246→Your plan should:
   247→- Cluster findings by ROOT CAUSE, not by dimension or detector
   248→- Give each cluster (epic) a clear thesis: one imperative sentence
   249→- Order epics by dependency: what must be done first for later work to make sense
   250→- Dismiss findings that don't make sense, are contradictory, or are false positives
   251→- Mark which epics are agent-safe (can be executed mechanically) vs need human judgment
   252→- Avoid creating work that contradicts other work in the plan
   253→- Be ambitious but realistic — aim to resolve all findings coherently
   254→
   255→Available directions for epics: delete, merge, flatten, enforce, simplify, decompose, extract, inline.
   256→
   257→Available plan tools (the agent executing your plan has access to these):
   258→- `desloppify plan queue` — view all items in priority order
   259→- `desloppify plan focus epic/<name>` — focus the queue on one epic
   260→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --permanent --note "why" --attest "..."` — permanently dismiss
   261→- `desloppify plan skip <id> --note "revisit later"` — temporarily defer
   262→- `desloppify plan done <id> --note "what I did" --attest "..."` — mark resolved
   263→- `desloppify plan move <id> top|bottom|before|after <target>` — reorder
   264→- `desloppify plan cluster show <name>` — inspect a cluster
   265→- `desloppify scan` — re-scan after making changes to verify progress
   266→- `desloppify show review --status open` — see all open review findings
   267→
   268→Your output defines the ENTIRE work plan. Findings not assigned to any epic
   269→will remain in the queue as individual items. Dismissed findings will be
   270→removed from the queue with your stated reason.
   271→
   272→Respond with a single JSON object matching this schema:
   273→{
   274→  "strategy_summary": "2-4 sentence narrative: what the meta-plan says, top priorities, current state",
   275→  "epics": [
   276→    {
   277→      "name": "slug-name",
   278→      "thesis": "imperative one-liner",
   279→      "direction": "delete|merge|flatten|enforce|simplify|decompose|extract|inline",
   280→      "root_cause": "why this cluster exists",
   281→      "finding_ids": ["id1", "id2"],
   282→      "dismissed": ["id3"],
   283→      "agent_safe": true,
   284→      "dependency_order": 1,
   285→      "action_steps": ["step 1", "step 2"],
   286→      "status": "pending"
   287→    }
   288→  ],
   289→  "dismissed_findings": [
   290→    {"finding_id": "id", "reason": "why this finding doesn't make sense"}
   291→  ],
   292→  "contradiction_notes": [
   293→    {"kept": "finding_id", "dismissed": "finding_id", "reason": "why"}
   294→  ],
   295→  "priority_rationale": "why the dependency_order is what it is"
   296→}
   297→"""
   298→
   299→
   300→def build_synthesis_prompt(si: SynthesisInput) -> str:
   301→    """Build the user-facing prompt content with all finding data."""
   302→    parts: list[str] = []
   303→
   304→    # Section: existing epics
   305→    if si.existing_epics:
   306→        parts.append("## Existing Epics (update these, don't recreate)")
   307→        for name, epic in sorted(si.existing_epics.items()):
   308→            status = epic.get("status", "pending")
   309→            thesis = epic.get("thesis", "")
   310→            direction = epic.get("direction", "")
   311→            fids = epic.get("finding_ids", [])
   312→            parts.append(
   313→                f"- {name} [{status}] ({direction}): {thesis}"
   314→                f"\n  Findings: {', '.join(fids[:10])}"
   315→                f"{'...' if len(fids) > 10 else ''}"
   316→            )
   317→        parts.append("")
   318→
   319→    # Section: what changed
   320→    if si.new_since_last:
   321→        parts.append(f"## New findings since last synthesis ({len(si.new_since_last)})")
   322→        for fid in sorted(si.new_since_last):
   323→            f = si.open_findings.get(fid, {})
   324→            parts.append(f"- {fid}: {f.get('summary', '(no summary)')}")
   325→        parts.append("")
   326→
   327→    if si.resolved_since_last:
   328→        parts.append(f"## Resolved since last synthesis ({len(si.resolved_since_last)})")
   329→        for fid in sorted(si.resolved_since_last):
   330→            parts.append(f"- {fid}")
   331→        parts.append("")
   332→
   333→    # Section: all open review findings
   334→    parts.append(f"## All open review findings ({len(si.open_findings)})")
   335→    for fid, f in sorted(si.open_findings.items()):
   336→        detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
   337→        suggestion = detail.get("suggestion", "")
   338→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
   339→        confidence = f.get("confidence", "medium")
   340→        file_path = f.get("file", "")
   341→        summary = f.get("summary", "")
   342→        parts.append(f"- [{confidence}] {fid}")
   343→        parts.append(f"  File: {file_path}")
   344→        if dimension:
   345→            parts.append(f"  Dimension: {dimension}")
   346→        parts.append(f"  Summary: {summary}")
   347→        if suggestion:
   348→            parts.append(f"  Suggestion: {suggestion}")
   349→    parts.append("")
   350→
   351→    # Section: dimension scores for context
   352→    if si.dimension_scores:
   353→        parts.append("## Dimension scores (context)")
   354→        for name, data in sorted(si.dimension_scores.items()):
   355→            if isinstance(data, dict):
   356→                score = data.get("score", "?")
   357→                strict = data.get("strict", score)
   358→                issues = data.get("issues", 0)
   359→                parts.append(f"- {name}: {score}% (strict: {strict}%, {issues} issues)")
   360→        parts.append("")
   361→
   362→    # Section: previously dismissed
   363→    if si.previously_dismissed:
   364→        parts.append(f"## Previously dismissed ({len(si.previously_dismissed)})")
   365→        parts.append("Maintain unless contradicted by new evidence.")
   366→        for fid in si.previously_dismissed:
   367→            parts.append(f"- {fid}")
   368→        parts.append("")
   369→
   370→    return "\n".join(parts)
   371→
   372→
   373→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   374→# Result parsing
   375→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   376→
   377→def parse_synthesis_result(raw: dict, valid_ids: set[str]) -> SynthesisResult:
   378→    """Parse and validate raw LLM output into a SynthesisResult.
   379→
   380→    Invalid finding IDs are silently dropped from epics and dismissals.
   381→    """
   382→    strategy_summary = str(raw.get("strategy_summary", ""))
   383→
   384→    epics: list[dict] = []
   385→    for raw_epic in raw.get("epics", []):
   386→        if not isinstance(raw_epic, dict):
   387→            continue
   388→        name = str(raw_epic.get("name", "")).strip()
   389→        if not name:
   390→            continue
   391→        # Validate direction
   392→        direction = str(raw_epic.get("direction", "simplify")).strip()
   393→        if direction not in VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS:
   394→            direction = "simplify"
   395→        # Filter to valid finding IDs
   396→        finding_ids = [
   397→            fid for fid in raw_epic.get("finding_ids", [])
   398→            if isinstance(fid, str) and fid in valid_ids
   399→        ]
   400→        dismissed = [
   401→            fid for fid in raw_epic.get("dismissed", [])
   402→            if isinstance(fid, str) and fid in valid_ids
   403→        ]
   404→        action_steps = [
   405→            str(s) for s in raw_epic.get("action_steps", [])
   406→            if isinstance(s, str)
   407→        ]
   408→
   409→        epics.append({
   410→            "name": name,
   411→            "thesis": str(raw_epic.get("thesis", "")),
   412→            "direction": direction,
   413→            "root_cause": str(raw_epic.get("root_cause", "")),
   414→            "finding_ids": finding_ids,
   415→            "dismissed": dismissed,
   416→            "agent_safe": bool(raw_epic.get("agent_safe", False)),
   417→            "dependency_order": int(raw_epic.get("dependency_order", 999)),
   418→            "action_steps": action_steps,
   419→            "status": str(raw_epic.get("status", "pending")),
   420→        })
   421→
   422→    dismissed_findings: list[DismissedFinding] = []
   423→    for d in raw.get("dismissed_findings", []):
   424→        if not isinstance(d, dict):
   425→            continue
   426→        fid = str(d.get("finding_id", ""))
   427→        if fid in valid_ids:
   428→            dismissed_findings.append(
   429→                DismissedFinding(finding_id=fid, reason=str(d.get("reason", "")))
   430→            )
   431→
   432→    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote] = []
   433→    for c in raw.get("contradiction_notes", []):
   434→        if not isinstance(c, dict):
   435→            continue
   436→        contradiction_notes.append(ContradictionNote(
   437→            kept=str(c.get("kept", "")),
   438→            dismissed=str(c.get("dismissed", "")),
   439→            reason=str(c.get("reason", "")),
   440→        ))
   441→
   442→    priority_rationale = str(raw.get("priority_rationale", ""))
   443→
   444→    return SynthesisResult(
   445→        strategy_summary=strategy_summary,
   446→        epics=epics,
   447→        dismissed_findings=dismissed_findings,
   448→        contradiction_notes=contradiction_notes,
   449→        priority_rationale=priority_rationale,
   450→    )
   451→
   452→
   453→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   454→# Plan mutation
   455→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   456→
   457→def apply_synthesis_to_plan(
   458→    plan: PlanModel,
   459→    state: StateModel,
   460→    synthesis: SynthesisResult,
   461→    *,
   462→    trigger: str = "manual",
   463→) -> SynthesisMutationResult:
   464→    """Apply parsed synthesis result to the living plan.
   465→
   466→    1. Creates/updates synthesis-clusters in plan["clusters"]
   467→    2. Marks dismissed findings as synthesized_out skips
   468→    3. Reorders queue_order to group epic members by dependency_order
   469→    4. Updates epic_synthesis_meta with snapshot hash
   470→    """
   471→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   472→    now = utc_now()
   473→    result = SynthesisMutationResult()
   474→    result.strategy_summary = synthesis.strategy_summary
   475→
   476→    clusters = plan["clusters"]
   477→    skipped: dict = plan["skipped"]
   478→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   479→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   480→    version = int(meta.get("version", 0)) + 1
   481→    result.synthesis_version = version
   482→
   483→    # --- Update/create synthesis-clusters ------------------------------------
   484→    for epic_data in sorted(synthesis.epics, key=lambda e: e.get("dependency_order", 999)):
   485→        raw_name = epic_data["name"]
   486→        epic_name = f"{EPIC_PREFIX}{raw_name}" if not raw_name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX) else raw_name
   487→
   488→        existing = clusters.get(epic_name)
   489→        if existing and existing.get("thesis"):
   490→            # Update existing synthesis-cluster
   491→            existing["thesis"] = epic_data["thesis"]
   492→            existing["direction"] = epic_data["direction"]
   493→            existing["root_cause"] = epic_data.get("root_cause", "")
   494→            existing["finding_ids"] = epic_data["finding_ids"]
   495→            existing["dismissed"] = epic_data.get("dismissed", [])
   496→            existing["agent_safe"] = epic_data.get("agent_safe", False)
   497→            existing["dependency_order"] = epic_data["dependency_order"]
   498→            existing["action_steps"] = epic_data.get("action_steps", [])
   499→            existing["updated_at"] = now
   500→            existing["synthesis_version"] = version
   501→            existing["description"] = epic_data["thesis"]
   502→            # Don't overwrite in_progress status from agent
   503→            if existing.get("status") != "in_progress":
   504→                existing["status"] = epic_data.get("status", "pending")
   505→            result.epics_updated += 1
   506→        else:
   507→            # Create new synthesis-cluster
   508→            cluster: Cluster = {
   509→                "name": epic_name,
   510→                "description": epic_data["thesis"],
   511→                "finding_ids": epic_data["finding_ids"],
   512→                "auto": True,
   513→                "cluster_key": f"epic::{epic_name}",
   514→                "action": f"desloppify plan focus {epic_name}",
   515→                "user_modified": False,
   516→                "created_at": now,
   517→                "updated_at": now,
   518→                # Epic fields
   519→                "thesis": epic_data["thesis"],
   520→                "direction": epic_data["direction"],
   521→                "root_cause": epic_data.get("root_cause", ""),
   522→                "supersedes": [],
   523→                "dismissed": epic_data.get("dismissed", []),
   524→                "agent_safe": epic_data.get("agent_safe", False),
   525→                "dependency_order": epic_data["dependency_order"],
   526→                "action_steps": epic_data.get("action_steps", []),
   527→                "source_clusters": [],
   528→                "status": epic_data.get("status", "pending"),
   529→                "synthesis_version": version,
   530→            }
   531→            clusters[epic_name] = cluster
   532→            result.epics_created += 1
   533→
   534→    # --- Dismiss findings ---------------------------------------------------
   535→    dismissed_ids: list[str] = []
   536→    for df in synthesis.dismissed_findings:
   537→        fid = df.finding_id
   538→        dismissed_ids.append(fid)
   539→        # Remove from queue, add to skipped as synthesized_out
   540→        if fid in order:
   541→            order.remove(fid)
   542→        skipped[fid] = {
   543→            "finding_id": fid,
   544→            "kind": "synthesized_out",
   545→            "reason": df.reason,
   546→            "note": f"Dismissed by epic synthesis v{version}",
   547→            "attestation": None,
   548→            "created_at": now,
   549→            "review_after": None,
   550→            "skipped_at_scan": int(state.get("scan_count", 0)),
   551→        }
   552→        result.findings_dismissed += 1
   553→
   554→    # Also handle per-epic dismissed lists
   555→    for epic_data in synthesis.epics:
   556→        for fid in epic_data.get("dismissed", []):
   557→            if fid not in dismissed_ids and fid in order:
   558→                order.remove(fid)
   559→                dismissed_ids.append(fid)
   560→                skipped[fid] = {
   561→                    "finding_id": fid,
   562→                    "kind": "synthesized_out",
   563→                    "reason": f"Dismissed by epic synthesis v{version}",
   564→                    "note": None,
   565→                    "attestation": None,
   566→                    "created_at": now,
   567→                    "review_after": None,
   568→                    "skipped_at_scan": int(state.get("scan_count", 0)),
   569→                }
   570→                result.findings_dismissed += 1
   571→
   572→    # --- Reorder queue: epic findings grouped by dependency_order -----------
   573→    epic_finding_ids: set[str] = set()
   574→    epic_ordered_ids: list[str] = []
   575→    for epic_data in sorted(synthesis.epics, key=lambda e: e.get("dependency_order", 999)):
   576→        for fid in epic_data["finding_ids"]:
   577→            if fid not in epic_finding_ids and fid not in dismissed_ids:
   578→                epic_finding_ids.add(fid)
   579→                epic_ordered_ids.append(fid)
   580→
   581→    # Rebuild order: epic items first (by dependency), then non-epic items in original order
   582→    non_epic_items = [fid for fid in order if fid not in epic_finding_ids]
   583→    # Insert epic items at the front (after synthesis::pending if present)
   584→    new_order: list[str] = []
   585→    # Keep synthesis::pending at front if present (it will be pruned next sync)
   586→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in non_epic_items:
   587→        non_epic_items.remove(SYNTHESIS_ID)
   588→    new_order.extend(epic_ordered_ids)
   589→    new_order.extend(non_epic_items)
   590→    order.clear()
   591→    order.extend(new_order)
   592→
   593→    # --- Update synthesis meta -----------------------------------------------
   594→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   595→    open_review_ids = sorted(
   596→        fid for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   597→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   598→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   599→    )
   600→
   601→    plan["epic_synthesis_meta"] = {
   602→        "synthesized_ids": open_review_ids,
   603→        "last_run": now,
   604→        "version": version,
   605→        "dismissed_ids": dismissed_ids,
   606→        "finding_snapshot_hash": current_hash,
   607→        "strategy_summary": synthesis.strategy_summary,
   608→        "trigger": trigger,
   609→    }
   610→    plan["updated"] = now
   611→
   612→    return result
   613→
   614→
   615→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   616→# Top-level orchestrator
   617→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   618→
   619→@dataclass
   620→class SynthesisDeps:
   621→    """Injectable dependencies for the synthesis engine."""
   622→
   623→    llm_call: Any = None  # Callable[[str, str], str] — (system, user) -> response
   624→
   625→
   626→def synthesize_epics(
   627→    plan: PlanModel,
   628→    state: StateModel,
   629→    *,
   630→    deps: SynthesisDeps | None = None,
   631→    dry_run: bool = False,
   632→    trigger: str = "manual",
   633→) -> SynthesisMutationResult:
   634→    """Run epic synthesis: collect input, call LLM, apply results.
   635→
   636→    If ``dry_run`` is True, collects input and builds prompt but does not
   637→    call the LLM or mutate the plan.
   638→
   639→    If ``deps.llm_call`` is None, returns a dry-run result with the prompt.
   640→    """
   641→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   642→    si = collect_synthesis_input(plan, state)
   643→
   644→    prompt = build_synthesis_prompt(si)
   645→    valid_ids = set(si.open_findings.keys())
   646→
   647→    if dry_run or deps is None or deps.llm_call is None:
   648→        result = SynthesisMutationResult(dry_run=True)
   649→        result.strategy_summary = f"[dry-run] Prompt built with {len(si.open_findings)} findings"
   650→        return result
   651→
   652→    # Call LLM
   653→    try:
   654→        raw_response = deps.llm_call(_SYNTHESIS_SYSTEM_PROMPT, prompt)
   655→        raw_json = json.loads(raw_response)
   656→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   657→        logger.error("Epic synthesis LLM response parse error: %s", exc)
   658→        result = SynthesisMutationResult()
   659→        result.strategy_summary = f"Synthesis failed: {exc}"
   660→        return result
   661→
   662→    synthesis = parse_synthesis_result(raw_json, valid_ids)
   663→    return apply_synthesis_to_plan(plan, state, synthesis, trigger=trigger)
   664→
   665→
   666→__all__ = [
   667→    "DismissedFinding",
   668→    "FINDING_ID_RE",
   669→    "SynthesisDeps",
   670→    "SynthesisInput",
   671→    "SynthesisMutationResult",
   672→    "SynthesisResult",
   673→    "apply_synthesis_to_plan",
   674→    "build_synthesis_prompt",
   675→    "collect_synthesis_input",
   676→    "detect_recurring_patterns",
   677→    "extract_finding_citations",
   678→    "last_real_review_timestamp",
   679→    "parse_synthesis_result",
   680→    "synthesize_epics",
   681→]
   682→
```

```
   630→        print(colorize("  - Did you defer contradictory findings before clustering?", "dim"))
   631→        print(colorize("  - What clusters did you create and why?", "dim"))
   632→        print(colorize("  - Explicit priority ordering: which cluster 1st, 2nd, 3rd and why?", "dim"))
   633→        print(colorize("  - What depends on what? What unblocks the most?", "dim"))
   634→        return
   635→    if len(report) < 100:
   636→        print(colorize(f"  Report too short: {len(report)} chars (minimum 100).", "red"))
   637→        print(colorize("  Explain what you organized, your priorities, and focus order.", "dim"))
   638→        return
   639→
   640→    stages = meta.setdefault("synthesis_stages", {})
   641→    stages["organize"] = {
   642→        "stage": "organize",
   643→        "report": report,
   644→        "cited_ids": [],
   645→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
   646→        "finding_count": len(manual_clusters),
   647→    }
   648→
   649→    # Jump-back: preserve or clear confirmation
   650→    if is_reuse and existing_stage and existing_stage.get("confirmed_at"):
   651→        stages["organize"]["confirmed_at"] = existing_stage["confirmed_at"]
   652→        stages["organize"]["confirmed_text"] = existing_stage.get("confirmed_text", "")
   653→    cleared = _cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages, "organize")
   654→
   655→    save_plan(plan)
   656→
   657→    append_log_entry(plan, "synthesis_organize", actor="user",
   658→                     detail={"cluster_count": len(manual_clusters), "reuse": is_reuse})
   659→    save_plan(plan)
   660→
   661→    print(colorize(
   662→        f"  Organize stage recorded: {len(manual_clusters)} enriched cluster(s).",
   663→        "green",
   664→    ))
   665→    if is_reuse:
   666→        print(colorize("  Organize data preserved (no changes).", "dim"))
   667→        if cleared:
   668→            print(colorize(f"  Cleared confirmations on: {', '.join(cleared)}", "dim"))
   669→    else:
   670→        print(colorize("  Now confirm the plan.", "yellow"))
   671→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan synthesize --confirm organize", "dim"))
   672→    # Show cluster summary
   673→    for name in manual_clusters:
   674→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(name, {})
   675→        steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
   676→        desc = cluster.get("description", "")
   677→        desc_str = f" \u2014 {desc}" if desc else ""
   678→        print(colorize(f"    {name}: {len(cluster.get('finding_ids', []))} findings, {len(steps)} steps{desc_str}", "dim"))
   679→
   680→    # Print the organize report prominently — this is the debrief
   681→    print()
   682→    print(colorize("  \u250c\u2500 Prioritized organization (share with user) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2510", "cyan"))
   683→    for line in report.strip().splitlines():
   684→        print(colorize(f"  \u2502 {line}", "cyan"))
   685→    print(colorize("  \u2514" + "\u2500" * 57 + "\u2518", "cyan"))
   686→    print()
   687→    print(colorize(
   688→        "  IMPORTANT: Present your prioritized organization to the user. Explain",
   689→        "yellow",
   690→    ))
   691→    print(colorize(
   692→        "  each cluster, why it exists, and your explicit priority ordering \u2014",
   693→        "yellow",
   694→    ))
   695→    print(colorize(
   696→        "  which cluster comes first, second, third, what depends on what,",
   697→        "yellow",
   698→    ))
   699→    print(colorize(
   700→        "  and why that ordering matters.",
   701→        "yellow",
   702→    ))

   704→
   705→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   706→# Completion
   707→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   708→
   709→def _cmd_synthesize_complete(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   710→    """Complete synthesis \u2014 requires organize stage (or confirm-existing path)."""
   711→    strategy: str | None = getattr(args, "strategy", None)
   712→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
   713→    plan = load_plan()
   714→
   715→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   716→        print(colorize("  synthesis::pending is not in the queue \u2014 nothing to complete.", "yellow"))
   717→        return
   718→
   719→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   720→    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
   721→
   722→    # Require organize stage confirmed
   723→    if "organize" not in stages:
   724→        if "observe" not in stages:
   725→            print(colorize("  Cannot complete: no stages done yet.", "red"))
   726→            print(colorize('  Start with: desloppify plan synthesize --stage observe --report "..."', "dim"))
   727→        else:
   728→            print(colorize("  Cannot complete: organize stage not done.", "red"))
   729→            gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
   730→            if gaps:
   731→                print(colorize(f"  {len(gaps)} cluster(s) still need enrichment:", "yellow"))
   732→                for name, missing in gaps:
   733→                    print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
   734→                print(colorize(
   735→                    '  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "..." --steps "step1" "step2"',
   736→                    "dim",
   737→                ))
   738→                print(colorize(
   739→                    f"  Then: {SYNTH_CMD_ORGANIZE}",
   740→                    "dim",
   741→                ))
   742→            else:
   743→                manual = _manual_clusters_with_findings(plan)
   744→                if manual:
   745→                    print(colorize("  Clusters are enriched. Record the organize stage first:", "dim"))
   746→                    print(colorize(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   747→                else:
   748→                    print(colorize("  Create enriched clusters first, then record organize:", "dim"))
   749→                    print(colorize(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   750→            if meta.get("strategy_summary"):
   751→                print(colorize('  Or fast-track: --confirm-existing --note "why plan is still valid" --strategy "..."', "dim"))
   752→        return
   753→
   754→    # Fold-confirm: auto-confirm organize if attestation provided
   755→    if not stages["organize"].get("confirmed_at"):
   756→        if attestation and len(attestation.strip()) >= _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
   757→            organize_clusters = [n for n in plan.get("clusters", {}) if not plan["clusters"][n].get("auto")]
   758→            validation_err = _validate_attestation(
   759→                attestation.strip(), "organize", cluster_names=organize_clusters,
   760→            )
   761→            if validation_err:
   762→                print(colorize(f"  {validation_err}", "red"))
   763→                return
   764→            organized, total, _ = _synthesis_coverage(plan)
   765→            stages["organize"]["confirmed_at"] = utc_now()
   766→            stages["organize"]["confirmed_text"] = attestation.strip()
   767→            save_plan(plan)
   768→            print(colorize("  \u2713 Organize auto-confirmed via --attestation.", "green"))
   769→        else:
   770→            print(colorize("  Cannot complete: organize stage not confirmed.", "red"))
   771→            print(colorize("  Run: desloppify plan synthesize --confirm organize", "dim"))
   772→            print(colorize("  Or pass --attestation to auto-confirm organize inline.", "dim"))
   773→            return
   774→
   775→    # Re-validate cluster enrichment at completion time (prevents bypassing
   776→    # organize gate by editing plan.json directly)
   777→    manual_clusters = _manual_clusters_with_findings(plan)
   778→    if not manual_clusters:
   779→        any_clusters = [
   780→            name for name, c in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   781→            if c.get("finding_ids")
   782→        ]
   783→        if not any_clusters:
   784→            print(colorize("  Cannot complete: no clusters with findings exist.", "red"))
   785→            print(colorize('  Create clusters: desloppify plan cluster create <name> --description "..."', "dim"))
   786→            return
   787→
   788→    gaps = _unenriched_clusters(plan)
   789→    if gaps:
   790→        print(colorize(f"  Cannot complete: {len(gaps)} cluster(s) still need enrichment.", "red"))
   791→        for name, missing in gaps:
   792→            print(colorize(f"    {name}: missing {', '.join(missing)}", "yellow"))
   793→        print(colorize(
   794→            '  Fix: desloppify plan cluster update <name> --description "..." --steps "step1" "step2"',
   795→            "dim",
   796→        ))
   797→        return
   798→
   799→    # Verify cluster coverage
   800→    organized, total, clusters = _synthesis_coverage(plan)
   801→
   802→    if total > 0 and organized == 0:
   803→        print(colorize("  Cannot complete: no findings have been organized into clusters.", "red"))
   804→        print(colorize(f"  {total} findings are waiting.", "dim"))
   805→        return
   806→
   807→    if total > 0 and organized < total:
   808→        remaining = total - organized
   809→        print(colorize(
   810→            f"  Warning: {remaining}/{total} findings are not yet in any cluster.",
   811→            "yellow",
   812→        ))
   813→
   814→    # Strategy required
   815→    if not strategy:
   816→        print(colorize("  --strategy is required.", "red"))
   817→        existing = meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   818→        if existing:
   819→            print(colorize(f"  Current strategy: {existing}", "dim"))
   820→            print(colorize('  Use --strategy "same" to keep it, or provide a new summary.', "dim"))
   821→        else:
   822→            print(colorize('  Provide --strategy "execution plan describing priorities, ordering, and verification approach"', "dim"))
   823→        return
   824→
   825→    # Strategy length check (unless "same") — 200 chars forces substantive content
   826→    if strategy.strip().lower() != "same" and len(strategy.strip()) < 200:
   827→        print(colorize(f"  Strategy too short: {len(strategy.strip())} chars (minimum 200).", "red"))
   828→        print(colorize("  The strategy should describe:", "dim"))
   829→        print(colorize("    - Execution order and priorities", "dim"))
   830→        print(colorize("    - What each cluster accomplishes", "dim"))
   831→        print(colorize("    - How to verify the work is correct", "dim"))
   832→        return
   833→
   834→    # Show summary
   835→    print(colorize("  Synthesis summary:", "bold"))
   836→    if "observe" in stages:
   837→        obs = stages["observe"]
   838→        print(colorize(f"    Observe: {obs.get('finding_count', '?')} findings analysed", "dim"))
   839→    if "reflect" in stages:
   840→        ref = stages["reflect"]
   841→        recurring = ref.get("recurring_dimensions", [])
   842→        if recurring:
   843→            print(colorize(f"    Reflect: {len(recurring)} recurring dimension(s)", "dim"))
   844→        else:
   845→            print(colorize("    Reflect: no recurring patterns", "dim"))
   846→    if "organize" in stages:
   847→        manual = _manual_clusters_with_findings(plan)
   848→        print(colorize(f"    Organize: {len(manual)} enriched cluster(s)", "dim"))
   849→        for name in manual:
   850→            cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(name, {})
   851→            steps = cluster.get("action_steps", [])
   852→            print(colorize(f"      {name}: {len(steps)} steps", "dim"))
   853→
   854→    organized, total, _ = _synthesis_coverage(plan)
   855→
   856→    # Jump-back guidance before committing
   857→    print()
   858→    print(colorize("  To revise an earlier stage: desloppify plan synthesize --stage <observe|reflect|organize>", "dim"))
   859→    print(colorize("  Pass --report to update, or omit to keep existing analysis.", "dim"))
   860→
   861→    append_log_entry(plan, "synthesis_complete", actor="user",
   862→                     detail={
   863→                         "strategy_len": len(strategy.strip()),
   864→                         "coverage": f"{organized}/{total}",
   865→                     })
   866→
   867→    _apply_completion(args, plan, strategy)
   868→
   869→
   870→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   871→# Confirm-existing (skip path)
   872→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   873→
   874→def _cmd_confirm_existing(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   875→    """Fast-track: confirm existing plan structure is still valid."""
   876→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   877→    strategy: str | None = getattr(args, "strategy", None)
   878→    confirmed: str | None = getattr(args, "confirmed", None)
   879→    plan = load_plan()
   880→
   881→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
   882→        print(colorize("  synthesis::pending is not in the queue — nothing to confirm.", "yellow"))
   883→        return
   884→
   885→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
   886→    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
   887→
   888→    # Require a prior completed synthesis — can't skip the full flow on first run
   889→    prior_strategy = meta.get("strategy_summary", "")
   890→    if not prior_strategy:
   891→        print(colorize("  Cannot confirm existing: no prior synthesis has been completed.", "red"))
   892→        print(colorize("  The full OBSERVE → REFLECT → ORGANIZE → COMMIT flow is required the first time.", "dim"))
   893→        print(colorize(f"  Create and enrich clusters, then: {SYNTH_CMD_ORGANIZE}", "dim"))
   894→        return
   895→
   896→    # Determine if this is a light-path (additions only) or full ceremony
   897→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   898→    state = runtime.state
   899→    si = collect_synthesis_input(plan, state)
```

```
  1001→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

  1004→
  1005→def _print_reflect_dashboard(si: object, plan: dict) -> None:
  1006→    """Show completed clusters, resolved findings, and recurring patterns."""
  1007→    # si is a SynthesisInput
  1008→    completed = getattr(si, "completed_clusters", [])
  1009→    resolved = getattr(si, "resolved_findings", {})
  1010→    open_findings = getattr(si, "open_findings", {})
  1011→
  1012→    if completed:
  1013→        print(colorize("\n  Previously completed clusters:", "cyan"))
  1014→        for c in completed[:10]:
  1015→            name = c.get("name", "?")
  1016→            count = len(c.get("finding_ids", []))
  1017→            thesis = c.get("thesis", "")
  1018→            print(f"    {name}: {count} findings")
  1019→            if thesis:
  1020→                print(colorize(f"      {thesis}", "dim"))
  1021→            for step in c.get("action_steps", [])[:3]:
  1022→                print(colorize(f"      - {step}", "dim"))
  1023→        if len(completed) > 10:
  1024→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(completed) - 10} more", "dim"))
  1025→
  1026→    if resolved:
  1027→        print(colorize(f"\n  Resolved findings since last synthesis: {len(resolved)}", "cyan"))
  1028→        for fid, f in sorted(resolved.items())[:10]:
  1029→            status = f.get("status", "")
  1030→            summary = f.get("summary", "")
  1031→            detail = f.get("detail", {}) if isinstance(f.get("detail"), dict) else {}
  1032→            dim = detail.get("dimension", "")
  1033→            print(f"    [{status}] [{dim}] {summary}")
  1034→            print(colorize(f"      {fid}", "dim"))
  1035→        if len(resolved) > 10:
  1036→            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(resolved) - 10} more", "dim"))
  1037→
  1038→    recurring = detect_recurring_patterns(open_findings, resolved)
  1039→    if recurring:
  1040→        print(colorize("\n  Recurring patterns detected:", "yellow"))
  1041→        for dim, info in sorted(recurring.items()):
  1042→            resolved_count = len(info["resolved"])
  1043→            open_count = len(info["open"])
  1044→            label = "potential loop" if open_count >= resolved_count else "root cause unaddressed"
  1045→            print(colorize(
  1046→                f"    {dim}: {resolved_count} resolved, {open_count} still open — {label}",
  1047→                "yellow",
  1048→            ))
  1049→    elif not completed and not resolved:
  1050→        print(colorize("\n  First synthesis — no prior work to compare against.", "dim"))
  1051→        print(colorize("  Focus your reflect report on your strategy:", "yellow"))
  1052→        print(colorize("  - How will you resolve contradictions you identified in observe?", "dim"))
  1053→        print(colorize("  - Which findings will you cluster together vs defer?", "dim"))
  1054→        print(colorize("  - What's the overall arc of work and why?", "dim"))
  1055→

  1057→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

  1060→
  1061→def _cmd_synthesize_dashboard(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
  1062→    """Default view: show findings, stage progress, and next command."""
  1063→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
  1064→    state = runtime.state
  1065→    plan = load_plan()
  1066→    si = collect_synthesis_input(plan, state)
  1067→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
  1068→    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
  1069→
  1070→    print(colorize("  Epic synthesis \u2014 manual", "bold"))
  1071→    print(colorize("  " + "\u2500" * 60, "dim"))
  1072→    print(f"  Open review findings: {len(si.open_findings)}")
  1073→    print(colorize("  Goal: identify contradictions, resolve them, then group the coherent", "cyan"))
  1074→    print(colorize("  remainder into clusters by root cause with action steps and priorities.", "cyan"))
  1075→    if si.existing_epics:
  1076→        print(f"  Existing epics: {len(si.existing_epics)}")
  1077→    if si.new_since_last:
  1078→        print(colorize(f"  New since last synthesis: {len(si.new_since_last)}", "yellow"))
  1079→        for fid in sorted(si.new_since_last):
  1080→            f = si.open_findings.get(fid, {})
  1081→            dim = ""
  1082→            detail = f.get("detail")
  1083→            if isinstance(detail, dict):
  1084→                dim = detail.get("dimension", "")
  1085→            dim_tag = f" ({dim})" if dim else ""
  1086→            print(colorize(f"    * [{short_finding_id(fid)}] {f.get('summary', '')}{dim_tag}", "yellow"))
  1087→    if si.resolved_since_last:
  1088→        print(f"  Resolved since last synthesis: {len(si.resolved_since_last)}")
  1089→
  1090→    # Stage progress (with enrichment gaps)
  1091→    print()
  1092→    _print_stage_progress(stages, plan)
  1093→    if meta.get("stage_refresh_required"):
```

```
  1215→def _find_cluster_for(fid: str, clusters: dict) -> str | None:
  1216→    """Return the cluster name containing *fid*, or None."""
  1217→    for name, c in clusters.items():
  1218→        if fid in c.get("finding_ids", []):
  1219→            return name
  1220→    return None
  1221→

  1223→def _count_log_activity_since(plan: dict, since: str) -> dict[str, int]:
  1224→    """Count execution log entries by action since *since* timestamp."""
  1225→    counts: dict[str, int] = defaultdict(int)
  1226→    for entry in plan.get("execution_log", []):
  1227→        if entry.get("timestamp", "") >= since:
  1228→            counts[entry.get("action", "unknown")] += 1
  1229→    return dict(counts)
  1230→

  1232→def _show_plan_summary(plan: dict, state: dict) -> None:
  1233→    """Print a compact plan rendering: clusters + queue order + coverage."""
  1234→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
  1235→    active = {
  1236→        n: c for n, c in clusters.items()
  1237→        if c.get("finding_ids") and not c.get("auto")
  1238→    }
  1239→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
  1240→
  1241→    if active:
  1242→        print(colorize(f"\n  Clusters ({len(active)}):", "bold"))
  1243→        for name, cluster in active.items():
  1244→            count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
  1245→            steps = len(cluster.get("action_steps", []))
  1246→            desc = (cluster.get("description") or "")[:60]
  1247→            print(f"    {name}: {count} items, {steps} steps — {desc}")
  1248→
  1249→    queue_order = [fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", []) if fid != SYNTHESIS_ID]
  1250→    if queue_order:
  1251→        show = min(15, len(queue_order))
  1252→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue order (first {show} of {len(queue_order)}):", "bold"))
  1253→        for i, fid in enumerate(queue_order[:show]):
  1254→            f = findings.get(fid, {})
  1255→            summary = (f.get("summary") or fid)[:60]
  1256→            detector = f.get("detector", "?")
  1257→            cn = _find_cluster_for(fid, active)
  1258→            print(f"    {i+1}. [{detector}] {summary}{f' ({cn})' if cn else ''}")
  1259→
  1260→    organized, total, _ = _synthesis_coverage(plan)
  1261→    pct = int(organized / total * 100) if total else 0
  1262→    print(colorize(f"\n  Coverage: {organized}/{total} in clusters ({pct}%)", "bold"))
  1263→

  1272→def _validate_attestation(
  1273→    attestation: str,
  1274→    stage: str,
  1275→    *,
  1276→    dimensions: list[str] | None = None,
  1277→    cluster_names: list[str] | None = None,
  1278→) -> str | None:
  1279→    """Return error message if attestation doesn't reference required data, else None."""
  1280→    text = attestation.lower()
  1281→
  1282→    if stage == "observe":
  1283→        if dimensions:
  1284→            found = [d for d in dimensions if d.lower().replace("_", " ") in text or d.lower() in text]
  1285→            if not found:
  1286→                dim_list = ", ".join(dimensions[:6])
  1287→                return f"Attestation must reference at least one dimension from the summary. Mention one of: {dim_list}"
  1288→
  1289→    elif stage == "reflect":
  1290→        refs: list[str] = []
  1291→        if dimensions:
  1292→            refs.extend(d for d in dimensions if d.lower().replace("_", " ") in text or d.lower() in text)
  1293→        if cluster_names:
  1294→            refs.extend(n for n in cluster_names if n.lower() in text)
  1295→        if not refs and (dimensions or cluster_names):
  1296→            hints = (dimensions or [])[:4] + (cluster_names or [])[:4]
  1297→            return f"Attestation must reference at least one dimension or cluster. Mention one of: {', '.join(hints)}"
  1298→
  1299→    elif stage == "organize":
  1300→        if cluster_names:
  1301→            found = [n for n in cluster_names if n.lower() in text]
  1302→            if not found:
  1303→                names = ", ".join(cluster_names[:6])
  1304→                return f"Attestation must reference at least one cluster from the plan. Mention one of: {names}"
  1305→
  1306→    return None
  1307→

  1309→def _confirm_observe(args: argparse.Namespace, plan: dict, stages: dict, attestation: str | None) -> None:

  1332→    for dim in dim_names:
  1333→        print(f"    {dim}: {by_dim[dim]} findings")
  1334→
  1335→    cited = obs.get("cited_ids", [])
  1336→    if cited:
  1337→        print(f"  You cited {len(cited)} finding IDs in your report.")
  1338→
  1339→    if not attestation or len(attestation.strip()) < _MIN_ATTESTATION_LEN:
  1340→        if attestation:

  1344→            ))
  1345→        print(colorize("\n  If satisfied, confirm:", "dim"))
  1346→        print(colorize('    desloppify plan synthesize --confirm observe --attestation "I have thoroughly reviewed..."', "dim"))
  1347→        print(colorize("  If not, continue reviewing findings before reflecting.", "dim"))
  1348→        return
  1349→
  1350→    # Validate attestation references actual data
  1351→    validation_err = _validate_attestation(attestation.strip(), "observe", dimensions=dim_names)
  1352→    if validation_err:

  1354→        return

  1358→    stages["observe"]["confirmed_text"] = attestation.strip()

  1361→                     detail={"attestation": attestation.strip()})
  1362→    save_plan(plan)

  1366→def _confirm_reflect(args: argparse.Namespace, plan: dict, stages: dict, attestation: str | None) -> None:
```

```
  1496→                     detail={
  1497→                         "attestation": attestation.strip(),
  1498→                         "coverage": f"{organized}/{total}",
  1499→                     })
  1500→    save_plan(plan)
  1501→    print(colorize(f'  ✓ Organize confirmed: "{attestation.strip()}"', "green"))
  1502→
  1503→
  1504→def _cmd_confirm_stage(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
  1505→    """Router for ``--confirm observe/reflect/organize``."""
  1506→    confirm_stage = getattr(args, "confirm", None)
  1507→    attestation = getattr(args, "attestation", None)
  1508→    plan = load_plan()
  1509→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
  1510→    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
  1511→
  1512→    if confirm_stage == "observe":
  1513→        _confirm_observe(args, plan, stages, attestation)
  1514→    elif confirm_stage == "reflect":
  1515→        _confirm_reflect(args, plan, stages, attestation)
  1516→    elif confirm_stage == "organize":
  1517→        _confirm_organize(args, plan, stages, attestation)
  1518→
  1519→
  1520→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1521→# Start (manual trigger)
  1522→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1523→
  1524→def _cmd_synthesize_start(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
  1525→    """Manually inject ``synthesis::pending`` into the queue and clear prior stages."""
  1526→    plan = load_plan()
  1527→    order: list[str] = plan.setdefault("queue_order", [])
  1528→
  1529→    if SYNTHESIS_ID in order:
  1530→        print(colorize("  synthesis::pending is already in the queue.", "yellow"))
  1531→        meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
  1532→        stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
  1533→        if stages:
  1534→            print(colorize(f"  {len(stages)} stage(s) in progress — clearing to restart.", "yellow"))
  1535→            meta["synthesis_stages"] = {}
  1536→            save_plan(plan)
  1537→            append_log_entry(plan, "synthesis_start", actor="user",
  1538→                             detail={"action": "restart", "cleared_stages": list(stages.keys())})
  1539→            save_plan(plan)
  1540→            print(colorize("  Stages cleared. Begin with observe:", "green"))
  1541→        else:
  1542→            print(colorize("  Begin with observe:", "green"))
  1543→        print(colorize(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_OBSERVE}", "dim"))
  1544→        return
  1545→
  1546→    # Inject at front
  1547→    order.insert(0, SYNTHESIS_ID)
  1548→    meta = plan.setdefault("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
  1549→    meta["synthesis_stages"] = {}
  1550→    save_plan(plan)
  1551→
  1552→    append_log_entry(plan, "synthesis_start", actor="user",
  1553→                     detail={"action": "start"})
  1554→    save_plan(plan)
  1555→
  1556→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
  1557→    si = collect_synthesis_input(plan, runtime.state)
  1558→    print(colorize("  Synthesis started.", "green"))
  1559→    print(f"  Open review findings: {len(si.open_findings)}")
  1560→    print(colorize("  Begin with observe:", "dim"))
  1561→    print(colorize(f"    {SYNTH_CMD_OBSERVE}", "dim"))
  1562→
  1563→
  1564→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1565→# Router
  1566→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  1567→
  1568→def cmd_plan_synthesize(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
  1569→    """Run epic synthesis: staged workflow OBSERVE → REFLECT → ORGANIZE → COMMIT."""
  1570→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
  1571→    state = runtime.state
  1572→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
  1573→        return
  1574→
  1575→    # Route: --start
  1576→    if getattr(args, "start", False):
  1577→        _cmd_synthesize_start(args)
  1578→        return
  1579→
  1580→    # Route: --confirm observe/reflect/organize
  1581→    if getattr(args, "confirm", None):
  1582→        _cmd_confirm_stage(args)
  1583→        return
  1584→
  1585→    # Route: --complete
  1586→    if getattr(args, "complete", False):
  1587→        _cmd_synthesize_complete(args)
  1588→        return
  1589→
  1590→    # Route: --confirm-existing
  1591→    if getattr(args, "confirm_existing", False):
  1592→        _cmd_confirm_existing(args)
  1593→        return
  1594→
  1595→    # Route: --stage observe/reflect/organize
  1596→    stage = getattr(args, "stage", None)
  1597→    if stage == "observe":
  1598→        _cmd_stage_observe(args)
  1599→        return
```

```
    24→def _print_pattern_hints() -> None:

    34→def _cmd_cluster_create(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    35→    name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    36→    description: str | None = getattr(args, "description", None)

    43→        return
    44→    append_log_entry(

    46→        detail={"description": description, "action": action},
    47→    )
    48→    save_plan(plan)
    49→    print(colorize(f"  Created cluster: {name}", "green"))
    50→
    51→
    52→def _cmd_cluster_add(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    53→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    54→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    55→        return
    56→
    57→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    58→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    59→
    60→    plan = load_plan()
    61→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    62→    if not finding_ids:
    63→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
    64→        _print_pattern_hints()
    65→        return
    66→    try:
    67→        count = add_to_cluster(plan, cluster_name, finding_ids)
    68→    except ValueError as ex:
    69→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    70→        return
    71→
    72→    # Check for overlap with other manual clusters
    73→    member_set = set(plan["clusters"][cluster_name].get("finding_ids", []))
    74→    for other_name, other_cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    75→        if other_name == cluster_name or other_cluster.get("auto"):
    76→            continue
    77→        other_ids = set(other_cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    78→        if not other_ids:
    79→            continue
    80→        overlap = member_set & other_ids
    81→        if len(overlap) > len(other_ids) * 0.5:
    82→            print(colorize(
    83→                f"  Warning: {len(overlap)} finding(s) also in cluster '{other_name}' "
    84→                f"({len(overlap)}/{len(other_ids)} = {int(len(overlap)/len(other_ids)*100)}% overlap).",
    85→                "yellow",
    86→            ))
    87→
    88→    append_log_entry(
    89→        plan, "cluster_add", finding_ids=finding_ids, cluster_name=cluster_name, actor="user",
    90→    )
    91→    save_plan(plan)
    92→    print(colorize(f"  Added {count} item(s) to cluster {cluster_name}.", "green"))

   165→def _print_cluster_member(idx: int, fid: str, finding: dict | None) -> None:
   166→    """Print a single cluster member line with optional finding details."""
   167→    print(f"    {idx}. {fid}")
   168→    if not finding:
   169→        return
   170→    file = finding.get("file", "")
   171→    lines = finding.get("detail", {}).get("lines", [])

   173→    if file:

   175→    summary = finding.get("summary", "")
   176→    if summary:
   177→        print(colorize(f"       {summary}", "dim"))

   180→def _load_findings_best_effort(args: argparse.Namespace) -> dict:
   181→    """Load findings from state, returning empty dict on failure."""

   183→    return rt.state.get("findings", {})

   185→
   186→def _cmd_cluster_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   187→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
   188→    plan = load_plan()
   189→    cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name)
   190→    if cluster is None:
   191→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist.", "red"))
   192→        return
   193→
   194→    # Header
   195→    auto_tag = "Auto-generated" if cluster.get("auto") else "Manual"
   196→    cluster_key = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   197→    key_type = f" ({cluster_key.split('::', 1)[0]})" if cluster_key else ""
   198→    print(colorize(f"  Cluster: {cluster_name}", "bold"))
   199→    print(colorize(f"  Type: {auto_tag}{key_type}", "dim"))
   200→    desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   201→    if desc:
   202→        print(colorize(f"  Description: {desc}", "dim"))
   203→    action = cluster.get("action") or ""
   204→    if action:
   205→        print(colorize(f"  Action: {action}", "dim"))
   206→
   207→    # Members
   208→    finding_ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   209→    if not finding_ids:
   210→        print(colorize("  Members: (none)", "dim"))
   211→    else:
   212→        findings = _load_findings_best_effort(args)
   213→        print(colorize(f"  Members ({len(finding_ids)}):", "dim"))
   214→        for idx, fid in enumerate(finding_ids, 1):
   215→            _print_cluster_member(idx, fid, findings.get(fid))
   216→
   217→    # Commands
   218→    print()
   219→    print(colorize("  Commands:", "dim"))
   220→    print(colorize(f'    Resolve all:  desloppify plan done "{cluster_name}" --note "<what>" --attest "..."', "dim"))
   221→    print(colorize(f"    Drill in:     desloppify next --cluster {cluster_name} --count 10", "dim"))
   222→    print(colorize(f"    Skip:         desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}", "dim"))
   223→
   224→
   225→def _cmd_cluster_list(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   226→    plan = load_plan()
   227→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   228→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   229→    if not clusters:
   230→        print("  No clusters defined.")
   231→        return
   232→    print(colorize("  Clusters:", "bold"))
   233→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   234→        member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   235→        desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
   236→        marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
   237→        desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
   238→        auto_tag = " [auto]" if cluster.get("auto") else ""
   239→        print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{auto_tag}{desc_str}{marker}")
   240→
   241→
   242→def _cmd_cluster_update(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   243→    """Update cluster description and/or action_steps."""
   244→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
   245→    description: str | None = getattr(args, "description", None)
   246→    steps: list[str] | None = getattr(args, "steps", None)
   247→
   248→    if description is None and steps is None:
   249→        print(colorize("  Nothing to update. Use --description and/or --steps.", "yellow"))
   250→        return
   251→
   252→    plan = load_plan()
   253→    cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name)
   254→    if cluster is None:
   255→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist.", "red"))
   256→        return
   257→
   258→    if description is not None:
   259→        cluster["description"] = description
   260→    if steps is not None:
   261→        cluster["action_steps"] = list(steps)
   262→        print(colorize(f"  Stored {len(steps)} action step(s):", "dim"))
   263→        for i, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
   264→            print(colorize(f"    {i}. {step}", "dim"))
   265→        if len(steps) == 0:
   266→            print(colorize("  Warning: 0 steps stored. Did you forget the --steps values?", "yellow"))
   267→        elif len(steps) == 1 and len(steps[0]) > 100:
   268→            print(colorize("  Warning: only 1 step stored and it's quite long. Check shell quoting.", "yellow"))
   269→    cluster["user_modified"] = True
   270→
   271→    from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
   272→
   273→    cluster["updated_at"] = utc_now()
   274→    append_log_entry(
   275→        plan, "cluster_update", cluster_name=cluster_name, actor="user",
   276→        detail={"description": description, "steps": steps},
   277→    )
   278→    save_plan(plan)
   279→    print(colorize(f"  Updated cluster: {cluster_name}", "green"))
   280→
   281→
   282→def _cmd_cluster_merge(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   283→    """Merge source cluster into target cluster."""
   284→    source: str = getattr(args, "source", "")
   285→    target: str = getattr(args, "target", "")
   286→
   287→    plan = load_plan()
   288→    try:
   289→        added, source_ids = merge_clusters(plan, source, target)
   290→    except ValueError as ex:
   291→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   292→        return
   293→
   294→    append_log_entry(
   295→        plan, "cluster_merge", finding_ids=source_ids,
   296→        cluster_name=target, actor="user",
   297→        detail={"source": source, "added": added},
   298→    )
   299→    save_plan(plan)
   300→    print(colorize(
   301→        f"  Merged cluster {source!r} into {target!r}: "
   302→        f"{added} finding(s) added, {len(source_ids)} total moved. Source deleted.",
   303→        "green",
   304→    ))
   305→
   306→
   307→def cmd_cluster_dispatch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   308→    """Route cluster subcommands."""
   309→    cluster_action = getattr(args, "cluster_action", None)
   310→    dispatch = {
   311→        "create": _cmd_cluster_create,
   312→        "add": _cmd_cluster_add,
   313→        "remove": _cmd_cluster_remove,
   314→        "delete": _cmd_cluster_delete,
   315→        "move": _cmd_cluster_move,
   316→        "show": _cmd_cluster_show,
   317→        "list": _cmd_cluster_list,
   318→        "update": _cmd_cluster_update,
   319→        "merge": _cmd_cluster_merge,
   320→    }
   321→    handler = dispatch.get(cluster_action)
   322→    if handler is None:
   323→        _cmd_cluster_list(args)
   324→        return
   325→    handler(args)
   326→
   327→
   328→__all__ = ["cmd_cluster_dispatch"]
   329→
```
